🟡 조건부 반박bone-regeneration
Comparing One-Stage Versus Two-Stage Ridge Splitting Procedures: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis
Khanum Z, Shobha ES, Nainoor N, Shaju N · 2024 sr+ma
First PRISMA SR+MA to directly compare one-stage vs two-stage ridge splitting for horizontal alveolar ridge augmentation (11 studies qualitative, 3 in meta-analysis, January 2000–December 2021).
1단계 vs 2단계 치조제 분할술을 직접 비교한 최초 PRISMA SR+MA(정성 11편, 메타분석 3편, 2000–2021년).
VS
Preliminary report on a staged ridge splitting technique for implant placement in the mandible: a technical note
Enislidis G, Wittwer G, Ewers R · 2006 case-report
Prospective technical note (n=5 consecutive patients, 6 long-span mandibular sites, 17 implants) describing a two-stage ridge-split where a first-stage buccal corticotomy predetermines the greenstick-fracture line before the ridge is split 40 days later.
전향적 기술노트(연속 5명, 하악 6부위, 17 임플란트): 1단계 협측 피질골절개(corticotomy)로 녹색골절 위치를 미리 결정한 뒤 40일 후 치조제를 분할하는 2단계 술식.
🟡 조건부 반박bone-regeneration
The effect of a barrier membrane on the incorporation of DBBM in experimental dehiscence defects at early implant placement. A preclinical study
Ellis R, Chen S, Muñoz Guzón FM, Darby I · 2024 animal
Split-mouth dog study (n=9, 5×3 mm standardized buccal dehiscence at early maxillary implant placement) comparing DBBM + resorbable collagen membrane vs DBBM alone, assessed histomorphometrically at 6 weeks.
개 9마리 분구강 실험(조기 식립 시 5×3 mm 협측 열개 결손): DBBM+교원막 vs DBBM 단독을 6주 조직형태계측으로 비교.
VS
Guided Bone Regeneration with Collagen Membranes and Particulate Graft Materials: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis
Bastian Wessing, Stefan Lettner, Werner · 2018 sr+ma
SR+MA (20 RCTs / prospective controlled studies, 1980–2014) evaluating collagen-membrane + particulate-graft GBR for lateral alveolar ridge augmentation, with meta-analysis of four procedural moderators: membrane crosslinking, implant timing, membrane fixation, and decortication.
SR+MA(RCT·전향적 대조연구 20편, 1980–2014): 콜라겐막 + 입자형 이식재 측방 치조제 GBR 평가, 4가지 술식 변수(막 가교화·식립 시기·막 고정·피질골 천공) 메타분석.
🔴 정면충돌bone-regeneration
Injectable platelet rich fibrin (i-PRF) versus platelet rich fibrin (PRF) both mixed with beta-tri calcium phosphate in bone regeneration using metacarpal bone defect in goats: micro CT comparative study
Assiri M, Almoallim H, Alhindi M, Alkind · 2026 animal
Animal experimental study (18 male Najdi goats, 72 critical-size metacarpal bone defects, 4 groups/goat: spontaneous healing, β-TCP alone, β-TCP+PRF, β-TCP+i-PRF; micro-CT at 2/5/8 weeks).
동물 실험 연구 (수컷 Najdi종 염소 18마리, 좌측 중수골에 72개 임계크기결손, 마리당 4군: 자연치유, β-삼인산칼슘(β-Tricalcium Phosphate, β-TCP) 단독, β-TCP+혈소판풍부피브린(Platelet-Rich Fibrin, PRF), β-TCP+주사형혈소판풍부피브린(injectable Platelet-Rich Fibrin, i-PRF); 2/5/8주 마이크로 전산화단층촬영(micro-CT) 평가).
VS
Platelet-rich fibrin combined with a particulate bone substitute versus guided bone regeneration in the damaged extraction socket: An in vivo study
Park JY, Hong KJ, Ko KA, Cha JK, Gruber · 2022 animal
Animal study (beagle dog, 2-wall alveolar defects, 3 groups, 8 weeks): sticky bone (i-PRF + DPBM) with or without a solid-PRF membrane was compared to GBR (DPBM + collagen membrane) for socket healing.
동물 연구 (비글견, 2벽성 치조 결손, 3군, 8주): 액상 혈소판풍부피브린 (i-Platelet-Rich Fibrin, i-PRF) + 탈단백우골 (Deproteinized Bovine Bone Mineral, DPBM)의 sticky bone ± solid-PRF 멤브레인을 골유도재생술 (Guided Bone Regeneration, GBR; DPBM+콜라겐막)과 비교.
🟡 조건부 반박bone-regeneration
A Histological and Clinical Evaluation of Long-Term Outcomes of Bovine Bone-Derived Xenografts in Oral Surgery: A Systematic Review
Inchingolo AM, Marinelli G, Trilli I, De · 2025 sr
Systematic review (PRISMA 2020, PROSPERO CRD420251111685; 11 included studies from 217 screened) evaluating long-term histological and clinical outcomes of bovine-derived xenografts (Bio-Oss/DBBM) across sinus augmentation, ridge augmentation, and alveolar ridge preservation.
체계적 문헌고찰 (PRISMA 2020, PROSPERO CRD420251111685; 217편 스크리닝 중 11편 최종 포함) — 상악동거상술·치조제증대·발치와보존(ARP) 전반에서 우골유래 이종골이식재(Bio-Oss/DBBM)의 장기 조직학적·임상적 결과를 평가.
VS
Ten-year follow-up in a maxillary sinus augmentation using anorganic bovine bone (Bio-Oss). A case report with histomorphometric evaluation
Stefano Sartori, M. Silvestri, F. Forni, · 2003 case-report
Single-patient case report with serial trephine biopsy histomorphometry at 8 months, 2 years, and 10 years after Bio-Oss-only maxillary sinus floor augmentation (MSFA) — one of very few human studies documenting long-term sinus graft remodeling trajectory.
단일 환자 증례보고: Bio-Oss 단독 상악동거상술 (Maxillary Sinus Floor Augmentation, MSFA) 후 8개월·2년·10년 시점 연속 트레핀 생검 (Trephine Biopsy) 조직형태계측 — 인체 장기 MSFA 리모델링 궤적을 기록한 매우 드문 연구.
🟡 조건부 반박immediate-implant
Immediate implant placement in infected extraction sockets: A scoping review of case selection, decontamination protocols, clinical outcomes and complications
Espada-Salgado F-M, Iuga M-M, Oprea B · 2026 sr
Scoping review (PRISMA-ScR, JBI methodology; 72 records screened, 24 clinical studies 2015–2025 included) mapping evidence on immediate implant placement (IIP) in infected extraction sockets.
스코핑 리뷰 (PRISMA-ScR/JBI 방법론; 72편 스크리닝, 2015–2025년 24편 임상연구 포함): 감염 발치와 즉시식립(IIP) 근거를 매핑.
VS
Comparison of Immediate Implantation into the Socket with and without Periapical Pathology: Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis
Pranckeviciene A, Vaitkeviciene I, Siudi · 2024 sr+ma
Systematic review and meta-analysis (109 publications screened, 22 full-text reviewed) comparing long-term clinical outcomes of immediate implant placement (IIP) into extraction sockets with vs without periapical pathology.
SR+MA (109편 검색, 22편 전문 검토): 치근단 병변 있는 발치와 즉시식립 vs 없는 발치와 즉시식립의 장기 임상 결과 비교.
🟡 조건부 반박immediate-implant
Immediate Early and Delayed Implants: A 2-Year Prospective Cohort Study of 131 Transmucosal Flapless Implants Placed in Sites With Different Pre-extractive Endodontic Infections
Prati C, Zamparini F, Pirani C, Gatto MR · 2017 prospective
Prospective cohort (n=85 patients, 131 flapless ZirTi implants), University of Bologna; pre-extractive endodontic diagnosis determined placement timing — acute abscess → Early (8-12 weeks), chronic lesion → Immediate, healthy → Delayed; 2-year follow-up.
전향적 코호트 (n=85명, 131 ZirTi 임플란트), 볼로냐대학교; 발치전 근관 진단으로 식립 타이밍 결정 — 급성 농양→조기 (8–12주), 만성 병변→즉시, 정상→지연; 2년 추적.
VS
Comparison of Immediate Implantation into the Socket with and without Periapical Pathology: Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis
Pranckeviciene A, Vaitkeviciene I, Siudi · 2024 sr+ma
Systematic review and meta-analysis (109 publications screened, 22 full-text reviewed) comparing long-term clinical outcomes of immediate implant placement (IIP) into extraction sockets with vs without periapical pathology.
SR+MA (109편 검색, 22편 전문 검토): 치근단 병변 있는 발치와 즉시식립 vs 없는 발치와 즉시식립의 장기 임상 결과 비교.

전향적 코호트 (n=85명, 131 ZirTi 임플란트), 볼로냐대학교; 발치전 근관 진단으로 식립 타이밍 결정 — 급성 농양→조기 (8–12주), 만성 병변→즉시, 정상→지연; 2년 추적.
VS
Immediate Implants Placed in Infected and Noninfected Sites After Atraumatic Tooth Extraction with Immediate Prosthetic Provisionalization: A 1-Year Prospective Cohort Study
Muñoz-Cámara M, Prados-Frutos JC, Fernán · 2020 prospective
Prospective cohort (n=100: 50 acute periapical infection, 50 control), University of Murcia, Spain; flapless IIP with immediate provisionalization, Biomet 3i implants, 1-year follow-up.
전향적 코호트 (n=100: 급성 치근단 감염 50명·대조 50명), 스페인 무르시아대학교; 무피판 즉시식립+즉시 프로비저널, Biomet 3i, 1년 추적.
🟡 조건부 반박immediate-implant
Outcomes of flapless immediate implants with low primary stability
Jang EJ, Park JA, Cha SH, Kim YM, Choi H · 2025 retrospective
Retrospective case series (n=16 flapless immediate SLA implants in 16 patients; all meeting strict low-stability criteria: insertion torque <10 Ncm AND mean initial ISQ <55; 6 implants produced no measurable ISQ at placement; single center, Inje University).
후향 증례군 (n=16, 무피판 즉시 SLA 임플란트; 식립토크 <10 Ncm 및 평균 초기 ISQ <55의 엄격한 저안정성 기준; 6개는 식립 시 ISQ 측정 불가; 단일기관 인제대).
VS
The Five Thread Guideline: A New Guideline for Predicting Primary Stability With Dental Implants
Robert Stanley · 2020 case-report
Case letter (3 immediate-placement cases) proposing the Five Thread Guideline (5-TG): a minimum of five implant threads must engage native bone — assessed via CBCT virtual planning with SIMPLANT 17 (bone rendering toggled off) — to predict adequate primary stability, supplemented by a "socket stabilization" concept of bi- or tri-socket wall engagement.
즉시식립 3증례 케이스 레터: CBCT 가상 계획 (SIMPLANT 17, 골 렌더링 끔)으로 임플란트 나사산 최소 5개가 원래 골에 접촉하는지 확인하는 '5-TG' 가이드라인과 격벽·소켓벽 2–3면 고정(socket stabilization) 개념 제안.
🔴 정면충돌immediate-implant
Immediate implant placement into fresh extraction sockets versus delayed implants into healed sockets: A systematic review and meta-analysis
Mello CC, Lemos CAA, Verri FR, Dos Santo · 2017 sr+ma
Systematic review and meta-analysis (PROSPERO CRD42016043309; 30 eligible studies, search through November 2016) of 3,049 implants in 1,435 patients (mean age 46.68 years, minimum 6-month follow-up), comparing immediate implant placement in fresh extraction sockets versus delayed implant placement in healed sockets.
체계적 문헌고찰+메타분석 (PROSPERO CRD42016043309; 적격 연구 30편, 2016년 11월까지 검색): 발치와 신선 소켓 3,049개 임플란트(환자 1,435명, 평균 46.68세, 최소 6개월 추적) — 즉시식립 (Immediate Implant Placement, IIP) vs 치유된 소켓 지연식립 비교.
VS
A systematic review on survival and success rates of implants placed immediately into fresh extraction sockets after at least 1 year
Niklaus P. Lang, Lui Pun, Ka Yee Lau, Ka · 2012 sr+ma
Systematic review of 46 prospective studies (mean follow-up 2.08 years) on Type I immediate implant placement into fresh extraction sockets, searched via MEDLINE/Cochrane Library (1991–July 2010).
전향적 연구 46편(평균 추적 2.08년) 대상 체계적 문헌고찰(Lang 2012, Clin Oral Implants Res) — 발치와 즉시식립(Type I immediate implant) 임플란트의 생존율·성공률을 MEDLINE/Cochrane Library 검색(1991–2010.7)으로 평가.
🔴 정면충돌↔ 상호immediate-implant
Immediate implant placement into fresh extraction sockets versus delayed implants into healed sockets: A systematic review and meta-analysis
Mello CC, Lemos CAA, Verri FR, Dos Santo · 2017 sr+ma
Systematic review and meta-analysis (PROSPERO CRD42016043309; 30 eligible studies, search through November 2016) of 3,049 implants in 1,435 patients (mean age 46.68 years, minimum 6-month follow-up), comparing immediate implant placement in fresh extraction sockets versus delayed implant placement in healed sockets.
체계적 문헌고찰+메타분석 (PROSPERO CRD42016043309; 적격 연구 30편, 2016년 11월까지 검색): 발치와 신선 소켓 3,049개 임플란트(환자 1,435명, 평균 46.68세, 최소 6개월 추적) — 즉시식립 (Immediate Implant Placement, IIP) vs 치유된 소켓 지연식립 비교.
VS
Comparison of clinical outcomes of immediate versus delayed placement of dental implants: A systematic review and meta-analysis
Garcia-Sanchez R, Dopico J, Kalemaj Z, B · 2022 sr+ma
Systematic review and meta-analysis addressing two focused questions on immediate versus delayed dental implant placement (search through November 2019): Q1 pooled 6 head-to-head RCTs comparing immediate vs delayed placement; Q2 pooled 53 studies (22 RCTs, 11 CCTs, 20 case series) of immediate placement alone.
즉시식립 (Immediate Implant Placement, IIP) vs 지연식립 (Delayed Implant Placement) 비교 체계적 문헌고찰+메타분석(2019년 11월까지 검색): Q1은 즉시-지연 head-to-head RCT 6편, Q2는 즉시식립 단독 53편(RCT 22+CCT 11+증례군 20)을 각각 풀링.
🔴 정면충돌immediate-implant
Immediate implant placement into fresh extraction sockets versus delayed implants into healed sockets: A systematic review and meta-analysis
Mello CC, Lemos CAA, Verri FR, Dos Santo · 2017 sr+ma
Systematic review and meta-analysis (PROSPERO CRD42016043309; 30 eligible studies, search through November 2016) of 3,049 implants in 1,435 patients (mean age 46.68 years, minimum 6-month follow-up), comparing immediate implant placement in fresh extraction sockets versus delayed implant placement in healed sockets.

VS
Interventions for replacing missing teeth: dental implants in fresh extraction sockets (immediate, immediate-delayed and delayed implants)
Marco Esposito, Maria Gabriella Grusovin · 2010 sr+ma
Cochrane SR+MA (7 RCTs included from 14 identified; Cochrane 2010) comparing immediate, immediate-delayed, and delayed implant placement timing in fresh extraction sockets.
Cochrane SR+MA (2010, 식별 14개 RCT 중 7개 포함) — 즉시·즉시지연·지연 식립 타이밍 비교.
🔴 정면충돌immediate-implant
Effect of undersized drilling on the stability of immediate tapered implants in the anterior maxillary sector. A randomized clinical trial
Sierra-Rebolledo A, Tariba-Forero D, Rio · 2021 rct
RCT (n=30 immediate 3.75×13 mm tapered implants, anterior maxilla; 16 conventional drilling vs 14 undersized drilling with 3.0 mm twist drill omitted) measuring primary stability by insertion torque and RFA/ISQ at insertion, 6 weeks, and 12 weeks.
RCT (즉시식립 테이퍼드 임플란트 n=30, 상악 전치부; 통상 드릴링 16 vs 축경 [undersized, 3.0 mm twist 드릴 생략] 14) — 삽입 토크와 RFA/ISQ를 삽입·6주·12주에 측정.
VS
Innovation of clockwise osseodensification technique for primary stability in dental implant: a low-density bone cadaveric study
Pawornwan Rittipakorn, Warisara Ouyyamwo · 2025 in-vitro
Paired-site cadaveric tibia study (9 formalin-fixed human tibiae, 40 Osstem TS III implants, D3/D4 low-density bone) comparing a novel clockwise osseodensification protocol (CW-OD, 800 rpm cutting direction) vs standard drilling (SD).
짝지음-부위 카데바 경골 연구 (인체 경골 9구, 임플란트 40개, D3/D4 저밀도골): 신규 시계방향 골밀도화 (Clockwise Osseodensification, CW-OD, 800 rpm 절삭 방향) vs 표준 드릴링 (SD) 비교.
🟡 조건부 반박bone-biology
Effect of occlusal hypofunction and its recovery on the three-dimensional architecture of mandibular alveolar bone in growing rats
Jia Liu, Zuo-lin Jin, Qiang Li · 2015 animal
Rat micro-CT experiment (n=48 growing male Sprague-Dawley rats; normal 24, hypofunction 12, recovery 12) using an anterior bite-raising appliance to induce mandibular posterior occlusal hypofunction for 2 weeks, followed by 2-week recovery after appliance removal.
성장기 수컷 흰쥐 48마리(정상 24·저작저하 12·회복 12) micro-CT 연구: 앞니 교합거상장치로 구치부 교합 저기능을 2주간 유도한 후 2주간 회복 관찰.
VS
Current perspectives of residual ridge resorption: Pathological activation of oral barrier osteoclasts
Takeru Kondo, Hiroaki Kajiya, Hiroshi Eg · 2022 narrative-review
Narrative review (J Prosthodont Res 2022, Tohoku University group) redefining residual ridge resorption (RRR) mechanistically: rather than simple disuse atrophy, RRR is driven by pathological activation of osteoclasts on the external (lateral) surface of the alveolar ridge, mediated by oral-barrier lymphocytes.
J Prosthodont Res 종설(2022, 도호쿠대) — 잔존 치조제 흡수(Residual Ridge Resorption, RRR)를 단순 폐용성 위축이 아닌 구강 점막 림프구가 매개하는 치조제 외측면 파골세포(osteoclast) 병적 활성화로 재정의했다.
🟡 조건부 반박bone-biology
Bone biology in aging periodontal and peri-implant tissues
Dalia Rasheed Issa, Giuseppe Intini, Dan · 2026 narrative-review
Narrative review (Periodontology 2000, Univ. of Pittsburgh) that organizes age-related bone biology as a framework linking periodontal disease, impaired healing, and peri-implant bone in older adults (PubMed search, no meta-analysis).
Periodontology 2000 종설(피츠버그대) — 노화 골 생물학을 hallmarks-of-aging 틀로 정리해 치주염·치유 저하·peri-implant 골을 노령 환자에서 하나로 연결(PubMed 검색, 메타분석 없음).
VS
Sclerostin is essential for alveolar bone loss in occlusal hypofunction
Yang Xu, Lufei Wang, Yao Sun, Xianglong · 2016 animal
Rat split-mouth animal study (n=14 male Sprague-Dawley; unilateral maxillary molar extraction + incisor abrasion to create occlusal hypofunction, contralateral side as paired control; 8-week endpoint; micro-CT, TRAP histology, IHC).
수컷 흰쥐 split-mouth 연구(n=14, 편측 상악 대구치 발치+절치 마모로 교합저하 유도, 반대측 대조; 8주; micro-CT·TRAP 조직학·면역조직화학).
🔴 정면충돌implants
Crestal Bone Loss and Peri-Implant Conditions at Tissue-Level Implants: Influence of Prosthesis Type After 25 Years
Raabe C, Fonseca M, Roccuzzo A, Schimmel · 2026 retrospective
Single-center observational study, 147 patients / 233 tissue-level implants restored with single crowns (SC), splinted crowns (SP), or bridges (BR), evaluated at 10 and 25 years post-placement.
단일기관 관찰연구, 환자 147명·조직수준 임플란트 (tissue-level implant) 233개 — 단일치관 (single crown, SC)·연결치관 (splinted crown, SP)·브릿지 (bridge, BR)로 수복 후 10년·25년 추적.
VS
The influence of prosthetic designs on peri-implant bone loss: An AO/AAP systematic review and meta-analysis
Guo-Hao Lin, Eric Lee, Shayan Barootchi, · 2025 sr+ma
Joint AO/AAP SR+MA (PROSPERO CRD42023484513; 93 comparative studies, 85 in meta-analyses; 1980–2023; ≥12 months follow-up, ≥10 implants/group) evaluating the effect of implant prosthetic design variables on peri-implant marginal bone loss (MBL) and peri-implantitis risk.
AO/AAP 공동 SR+MA(PROSPERO CRD42023484513; 비교 연구 93편, 메타분석 85편; 1980–2023; 추적 ≥12개월, 군당 임플란트 ≥10개) — 임플란트 보철 디자인 변수가 임플란트 주위 변연골소실(Marginal Bone Loss, MBL)과 임플란트주위염 위험에 미치는 영향 평가.

The Effect of Implant Angulation and Clinical Risk Factors on Peri-implant Bone Changes: A 5-Year Retrospective Study
Donghyun Kim (Supervisor: Kwantae Noh) · 2026 retrospective
5-year single-center retrospective cohort (288 patients, 506 implants, mean follow-up 5.1 years) using CAD-derived 3D angulation — capturing both mesiodistal and buccolingual tilt — to examine peri-implant marginal bone loss (MBL); plaque scores ≤5% and routine occlusal adjustment controlled for microbial confounding.
5년 단일기관 후향 코호트(288명, 506개 임플란트, 평균 추적 5.1년): CAD 3D 각도 측정(근원심 + 협설 방향)으로 비축방향 로딩이 변연골 소실 (Marginal Bone Loss, MBL)에 미치는 영향 분석; 치태지수 ≤5% 환자만 포함, 교합조정으로 미생물 교란 최소화.
VS
Peri-Implant Bone Loss and Overload: A Systematic Review Focusing on Occlusal Analysis through Digital and Analogic Methods
Adolfo Di Fiore, Mattia Montagner, Stefa · 2022 sr
Systematic review (PRISMA; MEDLINE/Scopus/Cochrane 1985–2021; 492 records → 7 included; only clinical studies reporting quantitative occlusal contact or wear analysis; ROBINS-I bias appraisal) investigating whether occlusal overload — measured by digital or analogic quantitative methods — is associated with peri-implant bone loss.
체계적 고찰(PRISMA; MEDLINE·Scopus·Cochrane 1985–2021; 492편 → 7편 포함; 정량 교합접촉·교합마모 분석 보고 임상연구만 선택; ROBINS-I 비뚤림 평가): 교합 과부하와 임플란트주위 골소실(Peri-implant Bone Loss, PBL)의 관련성을 디지털·아날로그 정량 방법으로 검토하였다.

Bätz J, Syrigos S, Vorbeck M, Prüch E, C · 2019 in-vitro
Quasi-static bench experiment on ex vivo bovine trabecular bone cuboids comparing three total hip arthroplasty (THA) broach designs — compaction, blunt extraction, and sharp extraction — with broaching forces measured and micro-computed tomography (micro-CT) before and after testing.
준정적(quasi-static) 벤치 실험 — 생체외 소 해면골 육면체(bovine trabecular bone cuboid)에서 인공 고관절 치환술(Total Hip Arthroplasty, THA)용 브로치(broach) 3종(압착형 compaction, 무딘 추출형 blunt extraction, 예리한 추출형 sharp extraction)의 broaching 힘을 계측하고 시험 전후 마이크로 CT(micro-computed tomography, micro-CT)로 촬영.

A Novel Osseous Densification Approach in Implant Osteotomy Preparation to Increase Biomechanical Primary Stability, Bone Mineral Density, and Bone-to-Implant Contact
Huwais, Meyer · 2017 in-vitro
Foundational in vitro bench study (72 porcine tibial osteotomies, 3-arm design: standard drilling vs clockwise extraction vs counterclockwise OD, 2 implant diameters 4.1 and 6.0 mm): the same multi-fluted tapered bur run counterclockwise introduces osseodensification (OD).
기초 in vitro 벤치 연구 (돼지 경골 72 골삭제, 3군 설계: 표준 드릴링 vs 시계방향 추출 vs 반시계방향 골밀도화(OD), 임플란트 직경 4.1·6.0 mm): 같은 다중날 테이퍼형 버를 반시계방향으로 회전시키는 골밀도화(Osseodensification, OD)를 최초 도입한 논문.
🟡 조건부 반박implants
Short versus standard implants for single-crown restorations in the posterior region: A systematic review and meta-analysis
Xu X, Hu B, Xu Y, Liu Q, Ding H, Xu L · 2020 sr+ma
SR+MA (PRISMA/PROSPERO, 5 RCTs, short ≤6 mm vs standard >6 mm single crowns in the posterior region, searched to August 2019): short implants matched standard implants for short-term survival (RR 0.99, 95% CI 0.97–1.02, p=0.72) but showed significantly lower long-term survival (RR 0.94, 95% CI 0.90–0.99, p=0.01).
SR+MA(PRISMA/PROSPERO, 5 RCT, short ≤6 mm vs standard >6 mm, 구치부 단일 크라운, 검색 2019년 8월): 단기 생존율은 차이 없었으나(RR 0.99, p=0.72) 장기 생존율은 short가 유의하게 낮았다(RR 0.94, 95% CI 0.90–0.99, p=0.01).
VS
Short vs Longer Implants in the Posterior Alveolar Region (≥5-Year Observation): SR and Meta-Analysis
Xu X, Huang J, Fu X, Kuang Y, Yue H, Son · 2020 sr+ma
SR+MA (≥5-year observation, short vs longer implants in the posterior alveolar region, arch-stratified subgroup analysis): short implants in the mandible had survival comparable to longer implants, while short implants in the maxilla possibly survived at a lower rate.

Rika Singh, Shrikar R Desai, R G Shiva M · 2024 in-vitro
2D finite element analysis (ANSYS) of standard-diameter (3.75 mm) tapered and step implants in D4 posterior maxilla under immediate loading, isolating fixture length (6 mm vs 10 mm) under 100 N vertical and 45° oblique loads.
D4 상악 구치부·즉시하중 조건에서 표준직경(3.75 mm) 테이퍼드·스텝 임플란트의 픽스쳐 길이(6 mm vs 10 mm)를 분리 비교한 2D 유한요소해석(FEA, ANSYS), 100 N 수직 및 45° 사면 하중.
🔴 정면충돌implants
Autografting Tool with Enhanced Flute Profile and Methods of Use
Salah Huwais; Applicant Huwais IP Holdin · 2017 patent
PCT patent (WO 2017/124079 A1; Huwais IP Holding LLC) disclosing the engineering design of the Densah/Versah osseodensification rotary osteotome — a tapered multi-fluted bur whose core claimed improvement is a continuously negative rake angle producing dual-face flutes (cutting face + densifying face).
PCT 특허(WO 2017/124079 A1; Huwais IP Holding LLC) — Densah/Versah 골밀도화(Osseodensification, OD) 회전 osteotome 설계 공개. 핵심 개량은 연속 음의 레이크각(negative rake angle) flute으로, 각 flute에 cutting face + densifying face가 함께 존재.
VS
Condensing Screw for Implant Surgery with a Double-Spiral Structure (이중 스파이럴 구조의 임플란트 시술용 콘덴싱 스크류)
Kim Seong-ju (김성주); Applicant (주)해냄 (Hae · 2019 patent
Korean patent (KR 10-2304707 B1, registered 2021; applicant HaeNaem Co., inventor Kim Seong-ju) for a dental condensing screw with a double-spiral structure — compression thread plus bone-powder guide grooves plus a lower compression dome — enabling simultaneous lateral and apical bone densification in a single pass.
한국 등록특허(KR 10-2304707, 등록 2021, 발명자 김성주, 출원인 (주)해냄): 이중 스파이럴(압착나사산부 + 본파우더안내홈 + 하부압착돔) 구조로 측방 골치밀화와 근단부 골치밀화를 단일 기구로 수행.
🟡 조건부 반박implants
Survival of Short (≤6 mm) vs Longer (>6 mm) Implants in Posterior Partial Edentulism: Systematic Review of RCTs
Emfietzoglou R, Dereka X · 2024 sr
SR of RCTs (search to September 2023) comparing short (≤6 mm) vs longer (>6 mm) rough-surface implants in healthy adults with posterior partial edentulism, framed as an alternative to bone-regeneration procedures for resorbed ridges.
RCT 체계적 문헌고찰 (검색 2023년 9월까지) — 후방 부분 무치악의 흡수된 치조제에서 short(≤6 mm) vs 표준(>6 mm) 표면 거칠기 임플란트를 골재생술 대안으로 비교.

The rehabilitation of posterior atrophic maxilla: graftless short implant vs conventional long implant with sinus graft — SR and meta-analysis of RCTs
Chaware SH, Thakare V, Chaudhary R, Jank · 2021 sr+ma
SR+MA of 22 RCTs (667 patients, 1,595 implants: 767 short/graftless vs 835 long/grafted) comparing graftless short implants against conventional long implants with sinus graft for the posterior atrophic maxilla.
후방 위축 상악에서 무이식 단임플란트 vs 장임플란트+상악동이식을 비교한 RCT 22편 SR+MA(환자 667명, 임플란트 1,595개 — 단임플란트 767·장임플란트 835).
🔴 정면충돌implants
Condensing Screw for Implant Surgery with a Double-Spiral Structure (이중 스파이럴 구조의 임플란트 시술용 콘덴싱 스크류)
Kim Seong-ju (김성주); Applicant (주)해냄 (Hae · 2019 patent

VS
A Novel Osseous Densification Approach in Implant Osteotomy Preparation to Increase Biomechanical Primary Stability, Bone Mineral Density, and Bone-to-Implant Contact
Huwais, Meyer · 2017 in-vitro
Foundational in vitro bench study (72 porcine tibial osteotomies, 3-arm design: standard drilling vs clockwise extraction vs counterclockwise OD, 2 implant diameters 4.1 and 6.0 mm): the same multi-fluted tapered bur run counterclockwise introduces osseodensification (OD).
기초 in vitro 벤치 연구 (돼지 경골 72 골삭제, 3군 설계: 표준 드릴링 vs 시계방향 추출 vs 반시계방향 골밀도화(OD), 임플란트 직경 4.1·6.0 mm): 같은 다중날 테이퍼형 버를 반시계방향으로 회전시키는 골밀도화(Osseodensification, OD)를 최초 도입한 논문.
🟡 조건부 반박implants
A preclinical model links osseo-densification due to misfit and osseo-destruction due to stress/strain
Coyac BR, Leahy B, Salvi G, Hoffmann W, · 2019 animal
Preclinical in vivo animal study (rat maxillary extraction sockets; control = minimal implant–osteotomy misfit vs test = high misfit that osseo-densified the peri-implant bone), designed to link misfit, insertion torque, and implant stability to their short- and longer-term effects on peri-implant bone — the only held paper besides Trisi 2011 that *assigns* the mechanical exposure rather than binning it post-hoc.
전임상 생체 내 동물실험 (쥐 상악 발치와; 대조군 = 임플란트–골삭제 간 최소 미스핏(misfit) vs 시험군 = 주위골을 골밀도화 (Osseodensification, OD)시키는 고도 미스핏) — 미스핏·삽입 토크 (Insertion Torque, IT)·임플란트 안정성이 주위골에 미치는 단기·장기 효과를 연결하는 설계이며, 보유 논문 중 Trisi 2011과 함께 노출을 **사후 분류가 아니라 실험적으로 배정한** 단 두 편 중 하나다.
VS
High versus Low Implant Insertion Torque: A Histologic, Histomorphometric, and Biomechanical Study in the Sheep Mandible
Trisi P et al. · 2011 animal
Controlled animal histologic study (양 하악, 5마리, 임플란트 40개, 6주) comparing very high insertion torque (삽입 토크, HT = mean 110 Ncm) versus low torque (LT = mean 10 Ncm) using split-mouth design with sacrifice at 1–6 weeks.
양 하악 분할-구강 동물실험(5마리, 임플란트 40개, 6주): 과삽입토크(HT=평균 110 Ncm) vs 저토크(LT=10 Ncm) 조직학·생체역학 비교.

Combined effect of undersized surgical technique and axial compression on the primary implant stability and host bone architecture

🔴 정면충돌implants
The impact of a 1 mm interimplant distance on the interproximal crestal bone height: a case report with a 10-year follow-up and literature review
Morales Schwarz D, Szmukler-Moncler S, M · 2025 case-report
Case report + literature review (1 mm inter-implant distance, modern platform-switching bone-level implants with internal conical connection, subcrestal placement, 10-year follow-up): first clinical case of 1 mm IID to 10 years with quantified bone outcomes.
케이스보고 + 문헌고찰 (임플란트 간 거리(IID) 1 mm, 내부 원추형 연결부 + 플랫폼 스위칭 골수준 임플란트, 치조정하 식립, 10년 추적): 1 mm IID를 10년 정량 추적한 최초 임상 보고.

다기관 무작위대조시험(Randomized Controlled Trial, RCT) (환자 58명): 재건적 임플란트주위염(Peri-implantitis) 치료에서 전해세정법(Electrolytic Cleaning, GalvoSurge)과 과산화수소(Hydrogen Peroxide, HP) 보조 표면오염제거를 비교.

🔴 정면충돌implants/vitamin-d
The Relationship of Low-Serum Vitamin D and Early Dental Implant Failure.
Francis JR, Barber HD, Beals D, Siu T · 2024 prospective
Prospective cohort (174 implants / 109 patients) measuring serum vitamin D on the day of implant placement and following patients to restoration (~3–6 months) to assess correlation with early dental implant failure.
전향적 코호트(임플란트 174개/환자 109명): 임플란트 식립 당일 혈청 25-하이드록시비타민 D(serum 25-hydroxyvitamin D, 25(OH)D) 측정 후 보철 수복(약 3–6개월)까지 추적해 조기 임플란트 실패(Early Dental Implant Failure, EDIF)와의 상관관계를 분석.
VS
Is Vitamin D Deficiency a Risk Factor for Osseointegration of Dental Implants - A Prospective Study.
Mohsen KA, AbdEl-Raouf MN, Makram K, ElK · 2024 prospective
Single-center prospective observational study (53 patients, 143 implants, Egypt 2020–2023) testing whether pre-operative serum 25(OH)D category predicts early dental implant failure (EDIF) within 12 weeks.
단일기관 전향적 관찰 연구 (53명·임플란트 143개, 이집트 2020–2023) — 수술 전 혈청 25(OH)D 범주가 12주 이내 조기 임플란트 실패 (Early Dental Implant Failure, EDIF)를 예측하는지 검증.
🟡 조건부 반박implants/vitamin-d
Impact of Vitamin D on Osseointegration in Dental Implants: A Systematic Review of Human Studies.
Buzatu BLR, Buzatu R, Luca MM · 2024 sr
PRISMA systematic review of 7 human studies (2008–2021; 1,462 participants, 4,450 implants) providing the first human-only evidence map of vitamin D status and implant osseointegration.
PRISMA 체계적 고찰 (7편, 2008–2021; 참가자 1,462명·임플란트 4,450개) — 비타민 D 상태와 골유착(Osseointegration)의 사람 대상 근거를 처음으로 지도화한 리뷰.

Can Vitamin D Levels Influence Bone Metabolism and Osseointegration of Dental Implants? An Umbrella Review.
Tallon E, Macedo JP, Faria A, Tallon JM, · 2024 sr+ma
Umbrella review (PRISMA, JBI appraisal) of 5 systematic reviews (351 records screened; 8 qualifying primary studies, 2014–2021) examining vitamin D and dental-implant osseointegration.
우산형 종설(Umbrella Review, PRISMA·JBI)로 체계적 문헌고찰(Systematic Review, SR) 5편을 통합(검색 351건 → SR 5편 → 1차 연구 8편, 2014–2021년).

Customized three-dimensional-printed titanium mesh for alveolar bone augmentation: Retrospective consecutive case series
Soares KH, King C, Palkovics D, Perez R, · 2025 retrospective
Retrospective consecutive case series (n=9 patients, University of Florida Periodontology) using customized 3D-printed Ti mesh for GBR in combined large (n=5), vertical large (n=2), and combined medium (n=2) alveolar defects; introduced CBCT subtraction volumetric analysis and "augmentation efficacy" (cm³/mm) as outcome metrics.
후향적 연속 증례 시리즈(n=9명, 플로리다대학교 치주과): 맞춤형 3D 프린팅 Ti 메시로 복합 대형(n=5)·수직 대형(n=2)·복합 중형(n=2) 치조 결손에 골유도재생술(GBR) 시행; CBCT 감산 용적 분석 및 "증대 효율(Augmentation Efficacy, cm³/mm)" 지표 도입.

Retrospective consecutive case series (n=16 patients, single center) using a specific DMLS (Direct Metal Laser Sintering) micro-perforated grade-2 titanium mesh with only 2 vestibular fixation screws and pure autologous bone graft for GBR; CBCT subtraction analysis compared planned vs achieved augmentation (102 measurements, 3 operators).
후향적 연속 증례 시리즈(n=16명, 단일 기관): DMLS(Direct Metal Laser Sintering) 마이크로 퍼포레이션 grade-2 티타늄 메시, 2개 전정 고정 스크류, 순수 자가골 이식재로 골유도재생술(GBR) 시행; CBCT 감산 분석으로 계획 대비 실제 골증대 비교(102개 계측, 3명 측정자).

Vertical Ridge Augmentation Using Collagen Membrane and Tenting Screws in the Esthetic Zone: A Case Series

Case series (n=10 patients, esthetic zone, severe vertical defects) evaluating a Ti-mesh-free vertical ridge augmentation technique: titanium tenting screws placed at planned augmentation height, cross-linked resorbable collagen membrane draped over the screws, bone graft placed underneath.
증례 시리즈(n=10명, 심미 구역, 중증 수직 결손): 계획 증대 높이에 Ti 텐팅 스크류 식립 후 가교화(Cross-linked, XL) 흡수성 콜라겐막을 덮고 이식재를 삽입하는 Ti 메쉬 없는 수직골증대(Vertical Ridge Augmentation, VRA) 기법.

Does Length, Diameter, or Bone Quality Affect Primary and Secondary Stability in Self-Tapping Dental Implants?
Miguel Gómez-Polo, Rocío Ortega, Cristin · 2016 prospective
Prospective longitudinal clinical study of 88 self-tapping InHex implants in 63 partially edentulous patients, testing the effect of implant diameter (3.75 vs 4.25 mm), length (10 vs 11.5 mm), and Lekholm-Zarb bone quality on insertion torque (IT) and primary/secondary implant stability quotient (ISQ).

Relationship between Implant Stability Measurements Obtained by Insertion Torque and Resonance Frequency Analysis: A Systematic Review

PRISMA 체계적 문헌고찰(12편, 2,017편 검색): 삽입토크 (Insertion Torque, IT)와 임플란트 안정성 지수 (Implant Stability Quotient, ISQ)/공명주파수분석 (Resonance Frequency Analysis, RFA)의 상관관계 평가.

동물 연구 (Astra Tech EV 6mm 단경 임플란트 90개, 돼지 경골 해면골, 9군: 직경 3종 × 준비 기법 3종): 골밀도화 (Osseodensification, OD) 삽입 토크 이점이 임플란트 직경에 의존하는지 검증.
VS
OD vs Standard Drilling in Cadaveric Mandibles: Significant IT and Bone Density Increase (Mercier 2022)
Florian Mercier, Michel Bartala, Bruno E · 2022 in-vitro
In vitro cadaver study (21 formalin-fixed human mandibles, 58 implants) comparing osseodensification with Densah burs (OD, n=29) vs standard drilling (SD, n=29) on primary stability and peri-implant bone density.
In vitro 카데바 연구 (포르말린 고정 인체 하악골 21개, 임플란트 58개): Densah bur 골밀도화 (Osseodensification, OD, n=29) vs 표준 드릴링 (Standard Drilling, SD, n=29) 1차 안정성 및 주위 골밀도 비교.

In vitro evaluation of different implant systems and their influence on primary stability

In vitro study on fresh low-density bovine ribs comparing three osteotomy systems for a SIN Strong SW CM 3.5 × 8.5 mm implant — conventional drilling (SIN, control), osseodensification (VERSAH/Densah burs), and bone expander (MAXIMUS) — with micro-CT bone surface/volume ratio measured separately at cervical, body, and apical regions (n=5/group) and peak insertion torque (n=8/group).

RBH ≤5 mm에서 경치조골 상악동거상(Transcrestal Sinus Floor Elevation, TSFE) 후 합병증 및 조기 임플란트 실패 위험인자를 분석한 후향적 다기관 연구(이탈리아 7개 센터, n=430).

Therapeutic outcomes of non-grafted and platelet concentrations-grafted transcrestal maxillary sinus elevation (TSFE): SR+MA
Tianqi Guo, Karan Gulati, Ziyun Shen, Pi · 2020 sr+ma
SR+MA (18 studies: 13 non-grafted + 5 platelet-concentrate-grafted transcrestal sinus floor elevation, TSFE) comparing 1-year implant survival between graftless and platelet-concentrate-augmented approaches.
SR+MA (18연구, 비이식 13편·혈소판농축물 이식 5편): 경치조골 상악동거상술(Transcrestal Sinus Floor Elevation, TSFE) 1년 생존율 비교 연구.
🟡 조건부 반박sinus-lift/transcrestal
Effectiveness, Morbidity, and Costs of Transcrestal vs Lateral Sinus Floor Elevation by Residual Bone Height: RCT Re-Analysis

잔존 골 높이(Residual Bone Height, RBH) <4 mm vs ≥4 mm로 층화한 평행군 RCT 재분석(ITT: 경치조골 거상(Transcrestal Sinus Floor Elevation, tSFE) 29 vs 측방 거상(lSFE) 28).

🔴 정면충돌bone-regeneration/ridge-preservation
Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis of Hard Tissue Outcomes of Alveolar Ridge Preservation
Seyed Hossein Bassir, Muhanad Alhareky, · 2018 sr+ma
SR+MA (21 controlled studies) comparing alveolar ridge preservation (ARP) vs. extraction alone in human patients, using random-effects meta-analysis across seven dimensional outcomes with prespecified subgroup analyses for five modifiable factors.
체계적 문헌고찰 및 메타분석 (Systematic Review with Meta-Analysis, SR+MA, 21개 대조 연구): 치조제 보존술 (Alveolar Ridge Preservation, ARP) vs 발치 단독, 무작위 효과 모형, 7개 치수 결과 변수, 5개 사전 지정 하위군 분석.
VS
Is alveolar ridge preservation an overtreatment?
Nikos Mardas, Neil Macbeth, Nikolaos Don · 2023 narrative-review
Critical narrative review (Periodontology 2000, open access; Mardas, Macbeth, Donos, Jung, Zuercher) examining evidence for each of ARP's four canonical objectives: limiting dimensional change, promoting new bone formation, preserving soft tissue contour, and enabling implant placement without further augmentation.
비판적 종설 (Periodontology 2000 오픈액세스; Mardas 외): ARP의 네 가지 목표 — ①차원 변화 제한, ②신생골 형성, ③연조직/윤곽 유지, ④추가 증대 없이 임플란트 식립 — 각각의 근거를 검토.

Nikos Mardas, Neil Macbeth, Nikolaos Don · 2023 narrative-review
Critical narrative review (Periodontology 2000, open access; Mardas, Macbeth, Donos, Jung, Zuercher) examining evidence for each of ARP's four canonical objectives: limiting dimensional change, promoting new bone formation, preserving soft tissue contour, and enabling implant placement without further augmentation.
비판적 종설 (Periodontology 2000 오픈액세스; Mardas 외): ARP의 네 가지 목표 — ①차원 변화 제한, ②신생골 형성, ③연조직/윤곽 유지, ④추가 증대 없이 임플란트 식립 — 각각의 근거를 검토.
🔴 정면충돌bone-regeneration/ridge-preservation
Is there clinical evidence to support alveolar ridge preservation over extraction alone? A review of recent literature and case reports of late graft failure
Robert J. Adams · 2022 narrative-review
BDJ narrative review (single author) plus two illustrative case reports from a UK general practice, arguing that statistically significant dimensional preservation from ARP does not automatically translate into clinical patient benefit.
BDJ 서사적 고찰 + 영국 일반의 증례 2건: 치조제 보존술(Alveolar Ridge Preservation, ARP)의 통계적 치수 보존 효과가 임상적 환자 이득으로 자동 변환되지 않음을 지적.
VS
Effect of alveolar ridge preservation interventions following tooth extraction: A systematic review and meta-analysis
Gustavo Avila-Ortiz, Leandro Chambrone, · 2019 sr+ma
SR+MA (EFP/AO consensus reference, J Clin Periodontol 2019): 22 RCTs classified across 9 distinct ARP modalities (xenograft+sealing, allograft+sealing, alloplast±sealing, PRF/PRP, cell therapy, etc.) — the most comprehensive ARP modality framework available.
체계적 문헌고찰 및 메타분석 (Systematic Review with Meta-Analysis, SR+MA, EFP/AO consensus reference, J Clin Periodontol 2019): 22개 무작위 대조 시험 (RCT)을 9개 치조제 보존술 (Alveolar Ridge Preservation, ARP) 술식 분류체계로 정리.
🟡 조건부 반박bone-regeneration/ridge-preservation
Is there clinical evidence to support alveolar ridge preservation over extraction alone? A review of recent literature and case reports of late graft failure
Robert J. Adams · 2022 narrative-review
BDJ narrative review (single author) plus two illustrative case reports from a UK general practice, arguing that statistically significant dimensional preservation from ARP does not automatically translate into clinical patient benefit.
BDJ 서사적 고찰 + 영국 일반의 증례 2건: 치조제 보존술(Alveolar Ridge Preservation, ARP)의 통계적 치수 보존 효과가 임상적 환자 이득으로 자동 변환되지 않음을 지적.
VS
발치와 보존술(ARP) — 분류·재료 선택·치유기전·임플란트 충분성 종합
Synthesis (Damian Lee) · 2026 synthesis
Multi-paper synthesis (animal landmark, micro-CT, several RCTs, prospective split-mouth, narrative classification, retrospective cohorts, SR+MAs) on alveolar ridge preservation (ARP): ARP reduces but does not eliminate post-extraction ridge loss (untreated: ~50% horizontal, 30–40% vertical within 6–12 months; bundle bone loss is obligate and immediate implant placement alone cannot prevent it).
다편 종합 — 발치와 보존술(Alveolar Ridge Preservation, ARP)은 발치 후 치조제 손실을 줄이지만 없애지는 못함(무처치 시 수평 ~50%·수직 30–40% 손실; 다발골(bundle bone) 소실은 필연적이며 즉시식립 단독으로도 막지 못함).
🟡 조건부 반박bone-regeneration/ridge-preservation
Assessment of Soft and Hard Tissue Characteristics of Ridge Preservation at Molar Extraction Sites with Severe Periodontitis: A Randomized Controlled Trial
Haoyun Zhang, Yiping Wei, Tao Xu, Min Zh · 2022 rct
Registered RCT protocol (ChiCTR2200056335; BMC Oral Health 2022) targeting a sparse-evidence niche: 2-arm parallel design (n=70 powered; stage III/IV periodontitis patients requiring molar extraction, age >25, ≥3 mm socket bone) comparing alveolar ridge preservation against natural healing with multi-modal outcomes.
등록된 무작위대조시험 프로토콜(ChiCTR2200056335; BMC Oral Health 2022): 중증 치주염(stage III/IV) 대구치 발치가 필요한 환자(연령 >25세, 소켓 골 ≥3 mm)를 대상으로 치조제보존술(ARP) vs 자연치유를 비교하는 2군 평행 설계(n=70), 다중모달 결과 지표 포함.
VS
Is alveolar ridge preservation an overtreatment?
Nikos Mardas, Neil Macbeth, Nikolaos Don · 2023 narrative-review
Critical narrative review (Periodontology 2000, open access; Mardas, Macbeth, Donos, Jung, Zuercher) examining evidence for each of ARP's four canonical objectives: limiting dimensional change, promoting new bone formation, preserving soft tissue contour, and enabling implant placement without further augmentation.
비판적 종설 (Periodontology 2000 오픈액세스; Mardas 외): ARP의 네 가지 목표 — ①차원 변화 제한, ②신생골 형성, ③연조직/윤곽 유지, ④추가 증대 없이 임플란트 식립 — 각각의 근거를 검토.
🟡 조건부 반박bone-regeneration/ridge-preservation
Analysis of Soft Tissue Healing Over Socket Orifice Sealed with Platelet-Rich Fibrin Membrane
Manlerd R, Jirajariyavej B, Ruangsawasdi · 2025 rct
Prospective 3-arm RCT (n=45 extraction sockets, Mahidol University Bangkok) compared socket sealing with L-PRF membrane, collagen sponge, or spontaneous healing over 21 days.
전향적 3군 RCT (n=45 발치와, 마히돌대학 방콕): L-PRF막 실링 vs 콜라겐 스펀지 실링 vs 자연치유를 21일간 비교.
VS
Soft-Tissue Healing Assessment after Extraction and Socket Preservation Using Platelet-Rich Fibrin (PRF) in Smokers: A Single-Blinded, Randomized, Controlled Clinical Trial
Alrayyes Y, Aloraini S, Alkhalaf A, Alja · 2022 rct
Four-arm single-blind RCT (n=18 current heavy smokers, 40 upper molar sockets, King Saud University) comparing A-PRF alone, sticky bone+A-PRF, FDBA+collagen membrane, and resorbable collagen plug over 28 days.
흡연자 (≥10개비/일) 대상 4군 단일맹검 무작위 대조 시험 (Randomized Controlled Trial, RCT, n=18, 소켓 40개): 진행형 농축혈소판 섬유소 (Advanced Platelet-Rich Fibrin, A-PRF) 단독, 끈적골 (sticky bone)+A-PRF, 동결건조 동종골 (Freeze-Dried Bone Allograft, FDBA)+교차결합 콜라겐 막, 흡수성 콜라겐 플러그 비교.
🟡 조건부 반박bone-regeneration/ridge-preservation
Analysis of Soft Tissue Healing Over Socket Orifice Sealed with Platelet-Rich Fibrin Membrane

Sealing materials for post-extraction site: a systematic review and network meta-analysis
Massimo Del Fabbro, Grazia Tommasato, Pa · 2022 sr+ma
Network meta-analysis (12 RCTs, 312 sites) prepared as an Italian Society of Osseointegration (IAO) consensus paper — the first NMA focused exclusively on sealing materials (membrane, soft tissue graft, collagen matrix) rather than grafting materials for post-extraction socket ARP.
네트워크 메타분석 (Network Meta-Analysis, NMA, 12개 무작위 대조 시험, 312 부위): 이탈리아 골유착학회 (Italian Society of Osseointegration, IAO) consensus paper — 이식재 재료가 아닌 소켓 밀봉 재료 (막·연조직 이식·콜라겐 기질)에 초점을 맞춘 첫 NMA.
🟡 조건부 반박bone-regeneration/ridge-preservation
Post-extraction dimensional changes: A systematic review and meta-analysis
Emilio Couso-Queiruga, Sandra Stuhr, Mus · 2021 sr+ma

PRISMA 체계적 문헌고찰 및 메타분석 (Systematic Review with Meta-Analysis, SR+MA, PROSPERO 등록, 28편, 20편 풀링, J Clin Periodontol 2021): 치조제 보존술 (ARP) 미시행 시 자연치유로 발생하는 치조제 흡수 정량 — 비대구치·대구치, 임상·방사선 측정 층화.
VS
A systematic review of post-extractional alveolar hard and soft tissue dimensional changes in humans
Wah Lay Tan, Terry L. T. Wong, May C. M. · 2012 sr
Systematic review (EAO 5th Consensus Workshop, 20 human RCTs/prospective cohorts) using weighted means to quantify time-dependent post-extraction alveolar hard and soft tissue dimensional changes without preservation intervention.
체계적 문헌고찰 (유럽치주학회 (European Association for Osseointegration, EAO) 5차 합의 워크숍, 인체 RCT/전향 코호트 20편): 가중평균으로 보존 처치 없는 발치 후 경·연조직 치수 변화를 시점별로 정량화.

In-vitro 3-way factorial study (64 extracted maxillary premolars, MOD inlays; Trios 3 IOS → Ceramill CAD → 5-axis milling; n=8/subgroup) comparing zirconia material (4Y-TZP Zolid Gen-X vs 3Y/5Y-gradient IPS e.max ZirCAD Prime), resin cement (self-adhesive RelyX Unicem vs MDP-based Panavia F 2.0), and thermomechanical aging (24 h vs 1,200,000 cycles) on marginal and internal fit.
인비트로 3방향 요인설계 (발거 상악 소구치 64개, MOD 인레이; Trios 3 IOS → Ceramill CAD → 5축 밀링; 군당 n=8): 지르코니아 재료(4Y-TZP Zolid Gen-X vs 3Y/5Y 그라디언트 IPS e.max ZirCAD Prime), 레진 시멘트(자가접착 RelyX Unicem vs MDP 기반 Panavia F 2.0), 열기계적 노화(24시간 vs 120만 사이클)가 변연·내면 적합도에 미치는 영향 비교.

In Vitro Evaluation of the Performance of Self-Adhesive Resin Cements on Zirconia
Jiyoung Kwon, Hosung Lee, Hyun-Jung Kim, · 2026 in-vitro
In vitro study (J Funct Biomater 2026, n=180 sandblasted zirconia specimens, 10,000 thermocycles at 5–55°C) comparing shear bond strength of three self-adhesive resin cements (SARCs) with/without Z-Prime Plus primer.

Dynamic finite element modelling of the macaque mandible during a complete mastication gape cycle
Panagiotopoulou O, Robinson D, Iriarte-D · 2023 animal

Ambili Ravindran P, Rohit Raghavan, Kira · 2024 in-vitro

3D 유한요소분석(Finite Element Analysis, FEA): 내부 연결부 두 종류(star tube-in-tube vs Morse+내부 헥스) × 맞춤 어버트먼트 재료 4종(티타늄·Co-Cr·Co-Cr-S·지르코니아) 조합에서 축 방향(200 N) 및 사선(100 N, 45°) 하중 하 변연부 응력 분포 비교.

Mrunali Dahikar, Ashish Mandwe, Kulvinde · 2026 sr+ma
Umbrella review (PROSPERO CRD42023461029, PRISMA 2020) of 7 systematic reviews (2020–2024, 63 unique RCTs, 5 with meta-analysis) comparing bioceramic vs. epoxy resin-based sealers for postoperative pain after primary non-surgical root canal treatment, with de novo random-effects pooling of RCT-level data extracted from the included reviews.
체계적 문헌고찰(Systematic Review, SR) 7편(2020–2024년, 고유 무작위대조시험(Randomized Controlled Trial, RCT) 63편, 그중 5편 메타분석 포함)을 대상으로 원발성 비외과적 근관치료(Non-Surgical Root Canal Treatment, NSRCT) 후 바이오세라믹(bioceramic) 실러와 에폭시레진(epoxy resin) 실러 간 술후통증(postoperative pain)을 비교한 우산리뷰(umbrella review, PROSPERO CRD42023461029, PRISMA 2020)로, 포함된 SR에서 추출한 RCT 수준 자료로 자체 랜덤효과모델 메타분석(random-effects meta-analysis)을 새로 수행했다.

Single case report (n=1, 36-year-old male): a cracked maxillary first molar (#16) with reversible pulpitis and crack extending into the pulp chamber, treated in one visit due to patient relocation — microscopic RCT followed immediately by morphology-driven preparation technique (MDPT) and bonded lithium-disilicate (IPS e.max Press) occlusal veneer.
단일 증례 보고 (n=1, 36세 남성): 균열이 있고 가역성 치수염이 있는 상악 제1대구치(#16), 균열이 치수강까지 진행 — 환자 이주로 인해 단일 내원에 현미경 근관치료 + MDPT + 리튬 디실리케이트(IPS e.max Press) 교합 비니어 즉시 수복.

🔴 정면충돌cracked-tooth
Saving the split: An umbrella review on therapeutic approaches for cracked tooth syndrome
Supreet Kaur, Lakshmi Puzhankara, Neetha · 2026 sr
First umbrella review on cracked tooth syndrome therapies (Manipal CDS, JCDE 29(1):11–19, 2026; PROSPERO CRD420250648720) searched 5 databases (PubMed, Cochrane, Scopus, EMBASE, Web of Science; 433 records) and synthesised 4 eligible SRs covering 30 primary studies (45 occurrences), with per-SR AMSTAR-2 confidence ranging High (Olivieri) to Critically low (Zhang, Mathew) and 17% CCA overlap.
균열치 증후군 치료법에 관한 첫 umbrella review(Manipal 치과대학, JCDE 29(1):11–19, 2026; PROSPERO CRD420250648720) — 5개 DB(PubMed·Cochrane·Scopus·EMBASE·Web of Science, 433건) 검색, 4편 SR(1차 연구 30편, 45회 인용) 통합; SR별 AMSTAR-2 confidence는 Olivieri High부터 Zhang·Mathew Critically low까지 상이, CCA 17%.
VS
Endodontic treatment in the management of cracked tooth
Kwak Youngjun · 2024 narrative-review
Narrative practitioner review (KDA Journal 2024, Korean) addressing the indications and timing of endodontic treatment in cracked tooth management, based on author clinical experience and literature.
균열치 관리에서 근관치료의 적응증과 시점을 다룬 KDA 한국어 임상 총설(2024)로, 저자의 임상 경험 및 문헌에 근거한다.
🟡 조건부 반박endodontics/irrigation
Different final irrigation protocols on postoperative pain and swelling following single-visit non-surgical root canal treatment in necrotic mandibular first molars: a randomized controlled trial
Zarei M, Javidi M, Farrokhi R, Tajdini Y · 2026 rct
Single-blind, parallel-group RCT (Mashhad University, n=45/15 per group) in asymptomatic necrotic mandibular first molars treated in a single visit with standardized ProTaper Gold + 5.25% NaOCl + 17% EDTA, comparing three final-irrigation delivery methods: conventional needle (CNI), side-vented needle (SNI), and passive ultrasonic irrigation (PUI).
마샤드대 단일맹검 병렬군 무작위 대조시험(n=45, 군당 15명) — 무증상 괴사 하악 제1대구치(Mandibular First Molar) 단일내원 근관치료(ProTaper Gold + 5.25% NaOCl + 17% EDTA)에서 최종 세정 방식 3가지 비교: 일반주사바늘(CNI), 측방배출바늘(SNI), 수동초음파세정(PUI).

Effectiveness of Irrigation Protocols in Endodontic Therapy: An Umbrella Review
Manuel J. Orozco-Gallego et al. · 2025 sr+ma
Umbrella review synthesizing 4 descriptive systematic reviews and 9 meta-analyses on irrigation protocol effectiveness in endodontic therapy — the highest-level synthesis currently available for this topic, with 8 of 13 included articles rated high methodological quality and low primary-study overlap between meta-analyses confirming independent evidence.
근관치료 세정 프로토콜 효과를 평가한 SR 4편+MA 9편의 우산 리뷰 — 현재 이 주제에서 가장 높은 수준의 합성 근거(포함 논문 13편 중 8편 고방법론 품질, MA 간 1차 연구 중복 낮음으로 독립 근거 확인).

Present status and future directions — irrigants and irrigation methods

Commissioned IEJ narrative review (2022) reframing endodontic paradigm: instrumentation serves primarily to provide canal access for irrigants, which bear the main responsibility for cleaning and disinfection.
IEJ 위탁 서술 리뷰(2022)로 근관치료 패러다임을 재정립: 기구조작은 주로 세정제가 접근할 수 있는 통로를 만들기 위한 것이며, 실질적 소독은 세정제가 담당.

GentleWave® 멀티소닉(Multisonic)과 초음파 활성화(Passive Ultrasonic Irrigation, PUI/PiezoFlow)의 다균종 바이오필름(Multispecies Biofilm) 제거 비교 PRISMA 체계적 고찰(발치치아 in vitro 3편; PROSPERO CRD42024583277) — 단균종 E. faecalis 모델은 임상 대표성 부족으로 제외.

Update on chelating agents in endodontic treatment: A systematic review

Systematic review of 56 studies (from 538, searched to Jan 2023) comparing five endodontic chelating agents — 17% EDTA, 9–18% HEDP (etidronic acid), citric acid, peracetic acid, and 7% maleic acid — across seven outcome axes including efficacy, erosion, cytotoxicity, and growth-factor release.
근관치료 킬레이트제 5종(17% EDTA·9–18% HEDP·구연산·퍼아세트산·7% 말레산)을 효능·침식성·세포독성 등 7개 축에서 비교한 56편 체계적 문헌고찰(538편 중 선별, 2023년 1월 기준).

🟡 조건부 반박endodontics/diagnosis

Igna A, Mircioagă D, Boariu M, Stratul Ș · 2022 narrative-review
Narrative review (Medicina 2022) examining pulp-testing methods specifically in the pediatric context — primary teeth and immature permanent teeth — where standard sensibility tests (EPT, thermal) are confounded by immature innervation, open apices, and poor patient cooperation.
Medicina 2022 서사 리뷰: 표준 감각 검사(전기치수검사, 온도 자극)가 미성숙 신경분포·개방 근단·협조 불량으로 결과가 왜곡되는 소아 치수검사에 특화하여, 유치 및 미성숙 영구치에서의 검사 선택을 안내.
VS
Diagnostic Accuracy of Pulp Vitality Tests and Pulp Sensibility Tests for Assessing Pulpal Health in Permanent Teeth: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis
Patro S, Meto A, Mohanty A, Chopra V, Mi · 2022 sr+ma
SR+MA (10 qualitative, 5 meta-analyzed) comparing diagnostic accuracy of pulp vitality tests (measuring blood flow) vs pulp sensibility tests (measuring nerve response) in permanent teeth, using pooled diagnostic odds ratio (DOR) and SROC analysis (QUADAS-2 quality appraisal).
SR+MA(정성 10편·메타분석 5편): 혈류 측정 생활력 검사(Vitality Test) vs 신경 반응 감각 검사(Sensibility Test)의 진단 정확도를 영구치 대상으로 비교 — 통합 진단오즈비(Diagnostic Odds Ratio, DOR)·SROC 분석, QUADAS-2 질 평가.
🟡 조건부 반박endodontics/diagnosis
On the causes of persistent apical periodontitis: a review
Nair PNR · 2006 narrative-review
Landmark narrative review (Nair 2006, Int Endod J) drawing on serial block-biopsy histobacteriology to systematize the six biological causes of persistent post-treatment apical periodontitis: intraradicular biofilm infection, extraradicular infection (periapical actinomycosis), foreign-body reaction, cholesterol crystals, true apical cyst, and scar tissue healing.
Nair (2006, Int Endod J) 연속 블록생검 조직세균학(histobacteriology)에 근거한 이정표적 종설: 근관치료 후 잔존 치근단치주염의 6가지 생물학적 원인 — ①근관 내 바이오필름 감염, ②근관 외 감염(치근단 방선균증), ③이물반응, ④콜레스테롤 결정, ⑤진성 근단 낭종, ⑥반흔 치유 — 을 정립.
VS
Apical Periodontitis in Vital and Nonvital Teeth: Clinical and Radiographic Features
Saeed Asgary, Anita Aminoshariae, Paul R · 2024 narrative-review
Narrative review (IEJ 2024) covering clinical and radiographic features of apical periodontitis (AP) in both vital and nonvital teeth, global AP prevalence (~52%), and an updated 8-diagnosis taxonomy integrating pulp status, percussion response, and radiographic findings.
IEJ 2024 서사 리뷰: 생활·비생활 치아 모두의 치근단 주위염(Apical Periodontitis, AP) 임상·방사선 소견, 전 세계 AP 유병률(~52%), 치수 상태·타진 반응·방사선 소견을 통합한 8범주 진단 분류 신규 제안.

🔴 정면충돌periodontics
Dysbiosis revisited: Understanding the role of the oral microbiome in the pathogenesis of gingivitis and periodontitis: A critical assessment
Scannapieco FA, Dongari-Bagtzoglou A. · 2021 narrative-review
Narrative critical-assessment commentary (J Periodontol, 2021) that revisits whether "microbial dysbiosis" correctly describes the pathogenesis of gingivitis and periodontitis, contrasting the periodontal literature against classical dysbiosis criteria from GI/skin disease.
치은염·치주염 병인론에서 "미생물 dysbiosis(불균형)" 개념이 실제로 타당한지 재검토한 비평 논평(J Periodontol, 2021) — 장·피부 질환의 고전적 dysbiosis 기준과 치주 문헌을 대조.
VS
Beyond the red complex and into more complexity: the polymicrobial synergy and dysbiosis (PSD) model of periodontal disease etiology
Hajishengallis G, Lamont RJ · 2012 narrative-review
Narrative review proposing the PSD (Polymicrobial Synergy and Dysbiosis) model to replace the reductionist "red complex" paradigm — periodontitis results from a synergistic dysbiotic community, with P. gingivalis acting as a keystone pathogen despite comprising <0.01% of total microbiota in mouse gavage experiments.
치주염의 원인을 'red complex' 특정 병원균 패러다임에서 벗어나 다균종 시너지·dysbiosis로 설명하는 PSD (Polymicrobial Synergy and Dysbiosis) 모델 제안 Narrative review — 쥐 실험에서 P. gingivalis는 전체 미생물의 <0.01%만으로도 dysbiosis 유도 가능.
🔴 정면충돌periodontics
Dysbiosis revisited: Understanding the role of the oral microbiome in the pathogenesis of gingivitis and periodontitis: A critical assessment
Scannapieco FA, Dongari-Bagtzoglou A. · 2021 narrative-review
Narrative critical-assessment commentary (J Periodontol, 2021) that revisits whether "microbial dysbiosis" correctly describes the pathogenesis of gingivitis and periodontitis, contrasting the periodontal literature against classical dysbiosis criteria from GI/skin disease.
치은염·치주염 병인론에서 "미생물 dysbiosis(불균형)" 개념이 실제로 타당한지 재검토한 비평 논평(J Periodontol, 2021) — 장·피부 질환의 고전적 dysbiosis 기준과 치주 문헌을 대조.
VS
Polymicrobial synergy and dysbiosis in inflammatory disease
Lamont RJ, Hajishengallis G · 2015 narrative-review
Narrative review formalizing the PSD (Polymicrobial Synergy and Dysbiosis) model with added molecular granularity: P. gingivalis–S. gordonii community formation is governed by a tightly regulated Ptk1/Ltp1/CdhR tyrosine phosphorylation cascade that also constrains P. gingivalis overgrowth; mouse co-infection with both species produces greater alveolar bone loss than either alone.
PSD(Polymicrobial Synergy and Dysbiosis) 모델을 분자 수준에서 심화한 Narrative review: P. gingivalis–S. gordonii 군집 형성이 Ptk1/Ltp1/CdhR 티로신 인산화 cascade로 조절되며, 두 균종 공동 감염이 단독 감염보다 치조골 소실 증가.
🟡 조건부 반박periodontics
The influence of periodontal status and serum biomarkers on salivary leptin levels in systemic lupus erythematosus patients
da Silva LA, Marques CPC, de Oliveira IC · 2022 cross-sectional
Case-control study (n=38 SLE patients, all female, mean age 39.6y vs n=29 healthy controls, mean age 46.3y; São Luís, Brazil) measuring periodontal parameters (PPD, CAL, BOP) and stimulated-saliva leptin, using a structural equation model (SEM) to test SLE status and periodontal status as independent predictors of salivary leptin.
케이스-대조군 연구 (전신홍반루푸스, Systemic Lupus Erythematosus, SLE 환자 38명(전원 여성, 평균 39.6세) vs 건강대조군 29명(평균 46.3세); 브라질 상루이스) — 치주 임상지표(치주낭깊이, Periodontal Probing Depth, PPD; 임상부착소실, Clinical Attachment Level, CAL; 탐침시출혈, Bleeding on Probing, BOP)와 자극 타액 렙틴(leptin)을 측정, 구조방정식모형(Structural Equation Model, SEM)으로 SLE와 치주상태를 독립변수로 검정.
VS
Relationship Between Rheumatoid Arthritis and Periodontal Disease—Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis
Dolcezza S, Flores-Fraile J, Lobo-Galind · 2024 sr+ma
SR+MA (PRISMA; PROSPERO CRD42020192179) of 6 RCTs (periodontal-treatment n=124 vs control n=131; ~2-month follow-up) testing whether non-surgical periodontal therapy reduces rheumatoid arthritis (RA) disease activity in patients with both conditions.
치주질환과 류마티스 관절염(Rheumatoid Arthritis, RA)을 함께 가진 환자에서 비외과적 치주치료가 RA 활성도를 낮추는지 평가한 RCT 6편(치료군 124 vs 대조군 131, 추적 약 2개월) SR+MA(PRISMA; PROSPERO CRD42020192179).

🟡 조건부 반박periodontics
Defining Porphyromonas gingivalis strains associated with periodontal disease
Murugaiyan V, Utreja S, Hovey KM, Sun Y, · 2024 cross-sectional
Cross-sectional strain-identification study (n=153 postmenopausal women, OsteoPerio/WHI ancillary cohort) validated a novel P. gingivalis intergenic spacer region (ISR) amplicon-sequencing method able to resolve strain identity, something 16S/shotgun microbiome methods cannot do.
단면 연구(cross-sectional), 폐경 후 여성 n=153(OsteoPerio/WHI 코호트) 대상, 16S/shotgun 분석법이 하지 못하는 P. gingivalis 균주 수준 식별을 가능케 하는 신규 intergenic spacer region (ISR, 유전자간 스페이서 부위) 시퀀싱법 검증.
VS
Breaking bad: Manipulation of the host response by Porphyromonas gingivalis
Hajishengallis G, Lamont RJ · 2014 narrative-review
Narrative review systematically detailing the molecular arsenal of P. gingivalis immune subversion at three levels: (1) epithelial invasion via FimA–β1-integrin with SerB dephosphorylation of NF-κBp65 suppressing CXCL8; (2) gingipain-mediated C3 degradation (all complement pathways blocked) while independently generating C5a; (3) C5aR–TLR2 crosstalk in macrophages suppressing iNOS killing and IL-12/IFN-γ via cAMP-PKA–GSK3β and ERK1/2-IRF-1.
P. gingivalis 숙주 면역 조작의 분자 기전 3단계 Narrative review: (1) FimA–β1-integrin 침투 + SerB CXCL8 억제; (2) gingipain의 C3 분해(보체 전 경로 차단) + 독자적 C5a 생성; (3) C5aR–TLR2 crosstalk으로 대식세포 iNOS·IL-12/IFN-γ 억제.
🟡 조건부 반박periodontics
Defining Porphyromonas gingivalis strains associated with periodontal disease

VS
Beyond the red complex and into more complexity: the polymicrobial synergy and dysbiosis (PSD) model of periodontal disease etiology
Hajishengallis G, Lamont RJ · 2012 narrative-review
Narrative review proposing the PSD (Polymicrobial Synergy and Dysbiosis) model to replace the reductionist "red complex" paradigm — periodontitis results from a synergistic dysbiotic community, with P. gingivalis acting as a keystone pathogen despite comprising <0.01% of total microbiota in mouse gavage experiments.
치주염의 원인을 'red complex' 특정 병원균 패러다임에서 벗어나 다균종 시너지·dysbiosis로 설명하는 PSD (Polymicrobial Synergy and Dysbiosis) 모델 제안 Narrative review — 쥐 실험에서 P. gingivalis는 전체 미생물의 <0.01%만으로도 dysbiosis 유도 가능.

Georgios Tsilingaridis, et al. · 2026 sr
EFP/EAPD-commissioned SR (269 studies, 47 countries; 22 large low-bias studies pooled for meta-analysis; PRISMA + ENTREQ; databases to January 2025) on biofilm-induced gingivitis in systemically healthy children and adolescents (<18 years), framed by the 2017 World Workshop classification.
EFP/EAPD 의뢰 SR (269편, 47개국; 대규모·저편향 22편 풀링; 2025년 1월까지 검색): 2017 World Workshop 분류 틀 내에서 전신질환 없는 18세 미만의 치면세균막유발 치은염 발생·결정요인·관리·OHRQoL 평가.
🔴 정면충돌interdental-cleaning
Efficacy of a water flosser compared to an interdental brush on gingival bleeding and gingival abrasion: A 4 week randomized controlled trial
Mancinelli-Lyle D, Van der Weijden FGA, · 2024 rct
Single-blind parallel 4-week RCT (n=78 young adults with moderate gingivitis, ACTA Amsterdam; WF n=40 vs IDB n=38) comparing water flosser (Waterpik) vs interdental brush (Interprox premium nano) as daily adjuncts to manual toothbrushing, measuring marginal (BOMP) and pocket (BOPP) bleeding and gingival abrasion (GAS).
단일맹검 평행 4주 RCT(n=78, 중등도 치은염, ACTA 암스테르담; WF n=40, IDB n=38): 물세정기(Waterpik) vs 치간칫솔(Interprox premium nano)을 수동칫솔질 일일 보조로 비교; 변연출혈지수(BOMP)·포켓출혈지수(BOPP)·치은마모(GAS) 측정.
VS
Oral Irrigator Versus Dental Floss in Orthodontic Patients: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis
Yiamwattana S, Kaomongkolgit R, Jaikumpu · 2025 sr+ma
Systematic review and meta-analysis (PROSPERO CRD42023395736; multi-database search to May 2024; 975 records screened → 4 eligible studies → 3 meta-analysed) comparing oral irrigator (OI) vs dental floss (DF), each adjunct to toothbrushing, in fixed-orthodontic patients.
SR+MA(PROSPERO CRD42023395736; 2024년 5월까지 다중 DB 검색; 975편 → 적격 4편 → 메타분석 3편): 고정 교정 환자에서 칫솔질에 구강세정기(Oral Irrigator, OI) vs 치실(DF)을 보조로 추가하는 비교.
🔴 정면충돌interdental-cleaning
Evaluating the hidden impact of interdental cleaning habits on maxillary anterior papilla integrity: a cross-sectional study
El Haddad S, Bukhary F, Alkhaldi A, Alot · 2026 cross-sectional
Cross-sectional study (n=69, ages 20–29, 87% toothpick users exclusively; no floss or interdental brush users) quantifying the effect of habitual wooden-toothpick use on maxillary anterior interdental papilla via the Papilla Presence Index (PPI).
단면연구(n=69, 20–29세, 87%가 나무 이쑤시개만 사용, 치실·치간칫솔 사용자 제외): 치간 세균막 제거 도구로 나무 이쑤시개만 쓰는 사람들의 상악 전치부 치간유두 손상을 치간유두 존재지수(Papilla Presence Index, PPI)로 평가.
VS
Efficacy of non-surgical treatment accompanied by professional toothbrushing in the treatment of chronic periodontitis in patients with type 2 diabetes mellitus: a randomized controlled clinical trial
Jae Young Lee, et al. · 2020 rct
Double-blind RCT (n=60 type 2 diabetes mellitus chronic-periodontitis patients; 3 arms: control, SRP, SRP + Watanabe-method professional toothbrushing once weekly ×5 visits; 12-week follow-up).
이중맹검 무작위 대조시험 (n=60, 2형 당뇨 만성 치주염; 3군: 대조·SRP·SRP+와타나베 이쑤시개법 전문 칫솔질 주 1회 ×5회; 12주).
🟡 조건부 반박interdental-cleaning
Case of Legionella pneumophila Serogroup 1 Infection Linked to Water Flosser, France
Slekovec C, Biguenet A, Jeanvoine A, Hoc · 2026 case-report
Case report (n=1, Besançon University Hospital, France 2026; 48-yo IgG-Kappa myeloma patient admitted for autologous HSCT): personal water flosser brought from home, used with nonsterile tap water and not captured at admission, cultured positive for Legionella pneumophila serogroup 1 (Lp1) at 300 CFU/L while all hospital water sources were negative.
증례 보고(n=1, 프랑스 브장송 대학병원 2026; 48세 IgG-카파 골수종, 자가 조혈모세포이식(autologous Hematopoietic Stem Cell Transplantation, auto-HSCT) 입원): 집에서 가져온 워터플로서를 비멸균 수돗물로 사용했으며 입원 시 재고 파악이 안 됐음; 기기에서 Legionella pneumophila serogroup 1(Lp1) 300 CFU/L 검출, 병원 수원은 모두 음성.

🔴 정면충돌↔ 상호nccl
The association between occlusal factors and noncarious cervical lesions: A systematic review
Silva AG, Martins CC, Zina LG, Moreira A · 2013 sr
Systematic review across 6 databases (PubMed, Web of Science, Cochrane, Lilacs, ClinicalTrials, NIH; 1082 records → 9 included: 6 cross-sectional + 2 case-control + 1 clinical trial; Kappa 0.8) evaluating occlusal risk factors (ORF) for NCCLs in adults.
6개 DB 체계적 문헌고찰(1082편 → 9편: 단면 6 + 환자대조 2 + 임상시험 1; Kappa 0.8): 성인 NCCL에서 교합위험인자(ORF: 편심위 간섭·교합력·조기접촉·유도 유형·편심위)의 역할 평가.
VS
Occlusal stress is involved in the formation of non-carious cervical lesions. A systematic review of abfraction
Duangthip D, Man A, Poon PH, Lo ECM, Chu · 2017 sr
Systematic review across PubMed, ISI Web of Science, and EMBASE (keyword "abfraction"; English only; 372 identified → 69 included: 31 clinical + 38 laboratory) evaluating whether occlusal stress is a mechanism in NCCL formation.
PubMed·Web of Science·EMBASE 체계적 문헌고찰("abfraction" 단일 키워드; 영어; 372편 → 69편: 임상 31 + 실험실 38): 교합응력이 NCCL 형성 기전인지를 평가.
🔴 정면충돌↔ 상호nccl

VS
Non-carious cervical lesions and occlusion: a systematic review of clinical studies
Senna P, Del Bel Cury A, Rosing C · 2012 sr
Systematic review of clinical studies on the NCCL–occlusion link (MEDLINE; 286 articles → 28 included: 3 prospective + 25 cross-sectional) — the first SR to focus specifically on clinical evidence for occlusion as an NCCL aetiological factor.
NCCL-교합 연관 임상연구 체계적 문헌고찰(MEDLINE; 286편 → 28편: 전향 3 + 단면 25) — 교합을 NCCL 병인인자로 평가한 임상연구만 다룬 최초 SR.
🟡 조건부 반박nccl
Abfraction Theory: Controversy Analysis, Scoping Review
Dioguardi M et al. · 2024 sr
PRISMA-ScR registered scoping review (INPLASY protocol; PubMed + Scopus; "abfraction" AND "NCCL"; 1449 articles identified → 6 included; ROBINS-I bias assessment) appraising whether occlusal loads cause abfraction lesions.
PRISMA-ScR 등록 scoping review(INPLASY 프로토콜; PubMed+Scopus; 1449편 → 6편 포함; ROBINS-I 비뚤림 평가): 교합부하가 abfraction 병변을 유발하는가를 평가.

🔴 정면충돌nccl
Scanning electron microscope characterization of noncarious cervical lesions in human teeth
Worawongvasu R · 2021 in-vitro
Ex vivo SEM study (JEOL JSM-6480 LV, x40–x20,000) of 10 extracted human premolars bearing NCCLs of varied morphology (concave, shallow, wedge-shaped, notched, irregular), one tooth per patient.
NCCL을 가진 소구치 발치치 10개를 JEOL JSM-6480 LV SEM(x40–x20,000)으로 검사한 ex vivo 서술적 연구 — 환자당 치아 1개, 다양한 형태(오목형·쐐기형·불규칙형 포함).
VS
Abfraction lesions: etiology, diagnosis, and treatment options
Nascimento MM et al. · 2016 narrative-review
Narrative review (University of Florida) concluding that despite decades of FEA and photoelastic modeling, clinical studies have failed to confirm a strong occlusal-loading → cervical-lesion association — the abfraction theory remains unproven — and adopting Grippo's pathodynamic schema.
플로리다대 narrative review: 수십 년간의 FEA·광탄성 모델링에도 불구하고 임상연구는 교합부하-치경부 병변 강한 연관을 확인하지 못했다고 결론 — abfraction 이론은 미입증 상태이며 Grippo pathodynamic schema를 채택.
🟡 조건부 반박caries
Fluoride varnish for preventing dental caries among children: a systematic review and meta-analysis of cost-effectiveness studies
Kumar RS, Gupta A, Sharda S, Bagepally B · 2026 sr+ma
SR+MA of 23 economic evaluation studies (836 screened, PROSPERO CRD42023483985) assessing cost-effectiveness of professional fluoride varnish vs other caries-preventive interventions for children in high-income countries.
SR+MA (경제성 평가 연구 23편, 836편 스크리닝, PROSPERO 등록): 고소득국 소아 우식예방에서 전문가 불소바니시(FV) vs 다른 예방중재의 비용효과성 평가.
VS
Fissure Seal or Fluoride Varnish? A Randomized Trial of Relative Effectiveness
Chestnutt IG, Playle R, Hutchings S, et · 2017 rct
Pragmatic RCT (n=1016 high-deprivation children, fissure sealant n=514 vs fluoride varnish n=501; 6-monthly applications, 36-month follow-up, 82.2% completion) comparing the two dominant occlusal caries-prevention technologies head-to-head.
사회적 취약계층 아동 1016명(점열구 실란트(Fissure Sealant, FS) 514명 vs 불소바니시(Fluoride Varnish, FV) 501명; 6개월 간격 도포, 36개월 추적, 완료율 82.2%) 대상 실용적 RCT.
🟡 조건부 반박tooth-whitening
Effect of at-home bleaching agents and concentrations on tooth sensitivity: A systematic review and network meta-analysis
Terra RMO, Favoreto MW, Morris T, Loguer · 2025 sr+ma
Bayesian NMA of 77 RCTs (50 meta-analyzed; GRADE low; PROSPERO CRD42021265220) comparing at-home bleaching concentration tiers (HP 2–4% to 14–16%; CP 5% to 20–22%) for tooth sensitivity risk and intensity.
베이지안 NMA (RCT 77편, 50편 메타분석; GRADE 낮음) — 자가미백 농도 티어(HP 2–4%~14–16%; CP 5%~20–22%) 간 치아 민감도 위험·강도 비교.
VS
At-home vs In-office Bleaching: An Updated Systematic Review and Meta-analysis
de Geus JL, Martins ACR, Reis A, Rezende · 2025 sr+ma
Updated SR+MA (32 RCTs including 6 new 2024 studies, 23 meta-analyzed; GRADE low certainty) comparing at-home vs in-office vital tooth bleaching across efficacy and adverse-effect outcomes.
갱신 SR+MA (RCT 32편, 2024년 신규 6편 포함, 23편 메타분석; GRADE 낮음)로 자가미백 대 진료실미백의 효과와 부작용을 비교.

Influence of Different Protocols on In-Office Bleaching: Whiteness Difference and Hydrogen Peroxide Penetration
Cordeiro DCF, Centenaro GG, Favoreto MW, · 2025 in-vitro
In vitro controlled study (n=154 extracted human premolars, 11 protocol groups × n=14) systematically comparing in-office bleaching application protocols (number and duration of gel applications) across two commercial products and two sessions 7 days apart.

Efficacy and Tooth Sensitivity of Low- Versus High-Concentration Hydrogen Peroxide for In-Office Bleaching: A Randomized Clinical Trial
Centenaro GG, Favoreto MW, Carneiro TS, · 2025 rct
Parallel double-blind RCT (n=140; 70 per group) comparing 6% vs 35% HP in-office bleaching over 3 sessions × 50 min with equivalence design, motivated by the EU restriction limiting in-office HP to 6%.
EU 진료실 HP 6% 규제 근거 마련을 위해 6% 대 35% HP 진료실 미백(3회 세션 × 50분)을 동등성 설계로 비교한 이중맹검 병렬 RCT(n=140; 군당 70명).

Polymerization shrinkage stress of contemporary dental composites: Comparison of two measurement methods
Szczesio-Wlodarczyk A, Garoushi S, Valli · 2024 in-vitro
In-vitro methodological comparison (Dental Materials J 2024; Turku/Lodz) of two shrinkage-stress measurement methods — photoelastic and contraction-forces — applied to the same 10 commercial resin composites (including EverX, G-ænial, Filtek One Bulk Fill, SDR flow+, Aura Bulk Fill).
In-vitro 방법론 비교(Dental Materials J 2024; Turku/Lodz): 상용 복합레진 10종(EverX, G-ænial, Filtek One Bulk Fill, SDR flow+, Aura Bulk Fill 포함)에 대해 photoelastic법과 수축력(Contraction-Forces)법을 동시 적용.

Polymerization shrinkage stress of resin-based dental materials: A systematic review and meta-analyses of composition strategies
Carine Tais Welter Meereis, Eliseu Aldri · 2018 sr+ma
SR+MA (62 in-vitro studies, 58 meta-analyzed; 7 databases, search to December 2016) of composition strategies for reducing polymerization shrinkage stress in resin-based dental materials, grouped into filler phase, coupling agent, and resin matrix modification.
SR+MA (in-vitro 연구 62편, 메타분석 58편; 7개 데이터베이스, 2016년 12월까지): 중합수축응력(Polymerization Shrinkage Stress, PSS) 감소를 위한 조성 전략을 충전제 상·커플링제·레진 기질 개질 3개 하위군으로 분류 분석.

Shikai Zhao, Zhiyi Zhu, Jian Yu, Chenmin · 2023 in-vitro
In vitro study (60 third molars, 4 solvent groups: water/ethanol/50% acetone/100% acetone) testing acetone wet-bonding (AWB) with Singlebond Universal under three aging conditions (24 h, 10,000 thermocycles, 1-month collagenase).
60개 제3대구치를 4개 용매군(물·에탄올·50% 아세톤·100% 아세톤)으로 나눠 Singlebond Universal로 접착 후 즉시/열순환 10,000회/콜라겐분해효소 1개월 노화 조건에서 미세인장결합강도·나노누출·in situ 자이모그래피·접촉각을 평가한 in vitro 연구.

A randomized clinical trial evaluating the success rate of ethanol wet bonding technique and two adhesives
Vajihesadat Mortazavi et al. · 2012 rct
1-year RCT (Dent Res J 2012; 36 NCCLs in 12 patients, Isfahan, Iran) comparing three adhesive strategies for cervical restorations: OptiBond FL (3-step etch-and-rinse), Clearfil SE Bond (2-step self-etch), and ethanol wet bonding (EWB) using the OptiBond FL adhesive component.
1년 RCT(Dent Res J 2012; NCCL 36개, 환자 12명, 이란 이스파한) — 치경부 수복에서 3가지 접착 전략 비교: OptiBond FL(3단계 산부식), Clearfil SE Bond(2단계 자가산부식), 에탄올 습윤접착법(EWB).

Yoshihara K, Yoshida Y, Hayakawa S, Naga · 2011 in-vitro
In-vitro XRD + TEM study comparing experimental 10-MDP primer with Clearfil SE Bond (commercial control) applied to human enamel and dentin under a common clinical protocol; concentration-series and active-rubbing experiments also performed.
실험용 10-MDP 프라이머와 Clearfil SE Bond(상용 대조군)를 인간 법랑질·상아질에 공통 임상 프로토콜로 적용한 in-vitro XRD·TEM 연구; 농도별 계열 실험 및 능동 문지르기 비교도 수행.
🟡 조건부 반박resin-bonding

In vitro study (54 single-canal mandibular premolars, 6 subgroups of n=9) comparing distilled-water control, 2% chlorhexidine (CHX), and 70% isopropyl alcohol (IA) root-dentin conditioning before fiber post cementation with TheraCem self-adhesive cement, testing immediate (24h) and late (post-thermocycling) push-out bond strength (PBS).
In vitro 연구 (하악 단근관 소구치 54개, 6개 하위군 각 n=9) — 파이버 포스트를 TheraCem 자가접착 레진시멘트(Self-Adhesive Resin Cement)로 합착하기 전 증류수 대조군 vs 2% 클로르헥시딘(Chlorhexidine, CHX) vs 70% 이소프로필알코올(Isopropyl Alcohol, IA)로 근관 상아질을 전처리 후 즉시(24시간)·후기(열순환 후) 푸시아웃 결합강도(Push-out Bond Strength, PBS) 비교.

🟡 조건부 반박local-anesthesia

Retrospective CBCT study (n=680 scans, 1,340 mandibular sides) in which the retromolar canal (RMC) was found in 16.12% of sides, most commonly in a vertical (type A1) course, with a mean diameter of 1.03 mm and distance of 15.1 mm from the second molar.
후향적 CBCT 연구 (n=680건, 하악 1,340측): 후구치관(RMC)이 16.12%에서 관찰되었으며, 주로 수직형(type A1)이고 평균 직경 1.03mm, 제2대구치까지 거리 15.1mm였다.

Efficacy of topical anesthetics of lidocaine, benzocaine, and EMLA in reducing pain during inferior alveolar nerve block in schoolchildren: a randomized controlled trial
M. Karkoutly, L. Alatassi, L. Azrak, N. · 2024 rct
Triple-blind 3-arm RCT (n=45 schoolchildren, 6–10 y, 15/arm) comparing 2-min application of 20% benzocaine gel, 8% lidocaine gel, and 5% EMLA cream before IANB, using FLACC behavioral scale, Wong-Baker FACES self-report, and pulse rate as outcomes.
삼중맹검 3군 RCT (n=45 학령기 소아, 6–10세, 군당 15명): IANB 전 20% benzocaine 겔·8% lidocaine 겔·5% EMLA 크림을 2분 도포 후 FLACC 행동 척도·Wong-Baker FACES·맥박으로 비교.
VS
Comparative Evaluation of Two Different Topical Anesthetic Agents against Pain Associated with Intraoral Injections in Pediatric Dental Patients
P. Subramanian, I. Kaur, K. Choubey, A. · 2023 rct
Two-arm RCT in 40 children (6–10 years, 20 per group) comparing 2% lignocaine jelly vs 20% benzocaine gel as topical anesthetic applied before inferior alveolar nerve block (IANB).
소아 40명(6–10세, 군당 20명)을 대상으로 IANB 전 2% 리그노카인 겔 vs 20% 벤조카인 겔을 비교한 2군 무작위대조시험.
🟡 조건부 반박local-anesthesia
Sensory innervation of mandibular teeth by the nerve to the mylohyoid: implications in local anesthesia
Stein P, Brueckner J, Milliner M · 2007 narrative-review
Narrative review (abstract-only) synthesizing dissection and clinical evidence on the nerve to the mylohyoid — traditionally classified as purely motor — as a source of accessory sensory innervation to mandibular teeth.
이설골설근신경(nerve to the mylohyoid)을 하악치아 부가 감각 지배의 원인으로 다룬 내러티브 리뷰(초록만 확보)로, 해부 연구와 임상 마취 실패 문헌을 종합.
VS
A Systematic Review of the Cervical Plexus Accessory Innervation and Its Role in Dental Anesthesia
Kim S, Uzbelger Feldman D, Yang J · 2016 sr
Systematic review screening 185 papers (1922–March 2015), retaining 44 on cervical plexus (C1–C4 branches: great auricular, transverse cervical, lesser occipital, supraclavicular nerves) as a source of accessory innervation to the mandible/lower face.
체계적 문헌고찰(185편 스크리닝 → 44편 포함, 1922–2015): 경부신경총(C1–C4 분지: 대이개신경, 횡경부신경, 소후두신경, 쇄골상신경)의 하악·하안면 부가신경 지배를 다룬 문헌 종합.
🟡 조건부 반박drug/systemic-disease
Diabetes Mellitus and Dental Implants: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis
Al Ansari Y, Shahwan H, Chrcanovic BR · 2022 sr+ma
SR+MA (89 publications, 68,290 implants; 5,510 diabetic vs 62,780 non-diabetic) evaluating dental implant failure odds and marginal bone loss (MBL) in diabetic vs non-diabetic patients, stratified by DM type and jaw location.
당뇨 vs 비당뇨 환자의 임플란트 실패 오즈 및 변연골소실(MBL)을 당뇨 유형·악골 위치별로 분류한 SR+MA (89편, 총 68,290개 임플란트; 당뇨 5,510 vs 비당뇨 62,780).
VS
Survival rate and peri-implant evaluation of immediately loaded dental implants in individuals with type 2 diabetes mellitus: a systematic review and meta-analysis
Carlos Alexandre Soares Andrade, João Lu · 2021 sr+ma
SR+MA (7 studies qualitative / 5 quantitative, first dedicated review on immediate loading in T2DM) searching 6 databases through January 2021; included studies n=4–108 patients, follow-up mostly 6–24 months.
SR+MA (정성 7편 / 정량 5편): 제2형 당뇨 환자의 즉시부하 임플란트에 특화된 첫 메타분석으로, 6개 데이터베이스 2021.1월까지 검색; 포함 연구 n=4–108명, 추적관찰 주로 6–24개월.
🔴 정면충돌drug/antibiotics
Interventions for replacing missing teeth: antibiotics at dental implant placement to prevent complications
Esposito M, Grusovin MG, Worthington HV · 2013 sr+ma
Cochrane systematic review + meta-analysis (search to 17 June 2013) of 6 RCTs, 1162 participants, comparing prophylactic antibiotics vs placebo/no antibiotics at dental implant placement.
코크란 체계적 문헌고찰+메타분석(2013년 6월 17일까지 검색): RCT 6편, 참여자 1162명 — 임플란트 식립 시 예방적 항생제 vs 위약/무투여 비교.

Is routine antibiotic prophylaxis warranted in dental implant surgery to prevent early implant failure? — a systematic review
Momand P, Naimi-Akbar A, Hultin M, Lund · 2024 sr+ma
SR+MA restricted to placebo-controlled, double-blinded, low/moderate-RoB RCTs only (7 trials, 1859 patients, 3014 implants; PROSPERO CRD42021292610) evaluating preoperative antibiotic prophylaxis vs placebo for early implant failure in overall healthy patients.
위약대조 이중맹검 RCT 7편만 포함한 SR+MA(환자 1859명/임플란트 3014개; PROSPERO CRD42021292610): 기존 SR+MA들이 비맹검·고위험-비뚤림 연구를 포함해 상충된 결론을 낸 한계를 방법론적으로 극복.
🟡 조건부 반박drug/antibiotics
Prophylactic Antibiotics for Prevention of Infective Endocarditis or Bacteremia in Pediatric Patients With Congenital Heart Disease Undergoing Dental Procedures: Systematic Review
Loper C, Baskette J, Fagan TR, Sardana D · 2025 sr
Systematic review (6 databases through April 2024, PROSPERO-registered) evaluating antibiotic prophylaxis for dental procedures in pediatric patients with congenital heart disease (CHD); only 3 case-control studies met inclusion from 1,774 screened, revealing a stark evidence vacuum.
선천성 심장질환(Congenital Heart Disease, CHD) 소아 대상 체계적 문헌고찰(6개 DB, 2024년 4월까지; 1,774편 선별 후 3편 증례-대조연구 포함): 예방적 항생제(Antibiotic Prophylaxis, AP) 근거가 소아 CHD에서 성인보다 더욱 취약함을 최초로 정량화.
VS

Sperotto F, France K, Gobbo M, et al. · 2024 sr+ma
SR+MA (JAMA Cardiology 2024, n=1,152,345 IE cases) risk-stratified analysis of antibiotic prophylaxis efficacy after invasive dental procedures across high-, moderate-, low-, and unknown-risk cardiac patient groups.
SR+MA (JAMA Cardiology 2024, n=1,152,345 IE 사례): 침습적 치과시술 후 항생제 예방 효과를 고위험·중간위험·저위험·불명 위험 심장질환군으로 층화 분석.
🟡 조건부 반박drug/antibiotics
Evaluation of the evidence on acetaminophen use and neurodevelopmental disorders using the Navigation Guide methodology
Diddier Prada, Beate Ritz, Ann Z. Bauer, · 2025 sr
Systematic review using the Navigation Guide environmental-health methodology (46 studies through Feb 2025) examining prenatal acetaminophen exposure and offspring neurodevelopmental disorders (ASD, ADHD); qualitative synthesis only (no meta-analysis) due to heterogeneity.
Navigation Guide 방법론 기반 체계적 리뷰(46편, 2025년 2월까지): 산전 아세트아미노펜(Acetaminophen) 노출과 자손의 신경발달장애(Neurodevelopmental Disorder, NDD; 자폐스펙트럼장애(ASD)·주의력결핍과잉행동장애(ADHD)) 연관성 연구 이질성으로 메타분석 불가, 정성 종합.
VS
Physiology of pregnancy and oral local anesthesia considerations
Xueer Zhou, Yunyu Zhong, Zijian Pan, Jia · 2023 narrative-review
Narrative review (West China Hospital of Stomatology, 182 studies from 546 screened) synthesizing pregnancy physiology and its implications for oral local anesthesia choice, technique, and timing.
임신 중 구강 국소마취 선택·기법·시기에 대한 내러티브 리뷰 (West China Hospital of Stomatology, 546편 스크리닝 후 182편 포함)로 임신 생리 변화와 마취 처방의 연결점을 정리.

Risk factors associated with early implant failure: A retrospective review
Yari A, Fasih P, Alborzi S, Nikzad H, Ro · 2023 retrospective
Retrospective single-center cohort (1323 implants / 738 patients, 4-year span, 53 early failures before final prosthetic loading, ~4% implant-level): multivariate logistic regression identified 6 independent predictors of early failure.
단일 기관 후향 코호트(임플란트 1323개 / 환자 738명, 4년간, 최종 보철 로딩 전 조기 실패 53건, 약 4%): 다변량 로지스틱회귀로 6가지 독립 위험인자 도출.
🟡 조건부 반박drug/analgesics
The effect of non-steroidal anti-inflammatory drugs on the osteogenic activity in osseointegration: a systematic review
Luo JD, Miller C, Jirjis T, Nasir M, Sha · 2018 sr
Systematic review (PRISMA; PROSPERO CRD42016051448; 1999-2018 search across Ovid/PubMed/Scopus/Web of Science; 875 titles → 79 full text → 13 included: 2 in vitro on titanium, 2 RCTs + 1 retrospective clinical, 7 animal; no meta-analysis due to heterogeneity) of NSAID effects on osseointegration.
체계적 문헌고찰 (PRISMA; PROSPERO CRD42016051448; 1999-2018 Ovid/PubMed/Scopus/Web of Science 검색; 875편 → 전문 79편 → 13편 포함: in vitro 2·RCT 2+후향 1·동물 7; 이질성으로 메타분석 없음)이 비스테로이드소염제 (Non-Steroidal Anti-Inflammatory Drugs, NSAIDs)의 골유착 (Osseointegration) 영향을 종합.

Effects of nonsteroidal anti-inflammatory drugs on osseointegration: a review
Gomes FIF, Aragão MGB, de Paulo Teixeira · 2015 sr
Structured review (Cochrane/PubMed/Medline, Jan 1982–Dec 2012; 360 references screened, 31 included: 2 randomized double-blind placebo-controlled clinical trials, 20 in vivo animal osseointegration studies, 9 in vitro osteoprogenitor-cell studies) of how NSAIDs affect osseointegration and dental implant survival.
NSAID가 골유착 (Osseointegration)과 임플란트 실패에 미치는 영향을 다룬 구조화 리뷰 (Cochrane/PubMed/Medline, 1982.1–2012.12; 360편 스크리닝 후 31편 포함: 무작위 이중맹검 위약대조 임상시험 2편, 동물 골유착 연구 20편, 시험관 골전구세포 (Osteoprogenitor cell) 연구 9편).
🟡 조건부 반박drug/analgesics
Randomized, Placebo-Controlled Pilot Study of Naproxen During Dental Implant Osseointegration
Kumchai H, Taub DI, Tomlinson RE · 2025 rct
Randomized, placebo-controlled pilot trial (n=12; maxillary implant patients) comparing naproxen 220 mg q8h for 7 days (n=7) against placebo (n=5), both with adjunctive acetaminophen, tracking implant stability (ISQ) and marginal bone loss over 16 weeks.
무작위 위약대조 파일럿 시험 (n=12; 상악 임플란트 환자)으로, 나프록센 (Naproxen) 220 mg 8시간마다 7일 투여군 (n=7)과 위약군 (n=5)을 아세트아미노펜 (Acetaminophen) 병용 하에 비교하며, 임플란트 안정성 (Implant Stability Quotient, ISQ)과 변연골소실 (Marginal Bone Loss, MBL)을 16주간 추적했다.

🟡 조건부 반박drug/mronj

Jin-Woo Kim et al.; Committee for the Ko · 2025 consensus

한국 5개 학회 공동 2025 MRONJ 포지션 스테이트먼트 — 체계적 문헌고찰 + 3라운드 델파이 합의, 60만 명 코호트(MRONJ 유병률 0.04%) 포함.

🟡 조건부 반박drug/anticoagulants
Comparative evaluation of surgicel and gelfoam in controlling post-extraction bleeding in patients on anticoagulant therapy: a clinical study
Al-Suliman W, Wehbeh I, Al-Hafyan S · 2025 prospective
Prospective single-blinded split-mouth clinical trial (n=40 anticoagulated patients — 20 aspirin, 20 warfarin; 80 matched extraction sockets) comparing Surgicel (oxidized cellulose) vs Gelfoam (gelatin sponge) as socket dressings without anticoagulant interruption.
전향적 단일맹검 분악 임상시험 (항응고제 유지 환자 40명 — 아스피린 20명·와파린 20명, 발치와 80곳): Surgicel(산화셀룰로오스) vs Gelfoam(젤라틴 스폰지) 비교.
VS
Bleeding and thromboembolic risk in patients under anticoagulant therapy receiving oral surgery: a systematic review
Madalina A. Moldovan, Laura V. Filip, Mi · 2023 sr
Systematic review (PRISMA 2020; 7 studies, 2000–2021) evaluating anticoagulant management during oral surgery under two regimens: continuous vs discontinuous anticoagulation, and continuous vs interruption with bridging therapy; primary procedure was tooth extraction.
PRISMA 2020 체계적 고찰(7편, 2000–2021): 구강외과 시 항응고제 지속 vs 중단, 지속 vs 브리징 요법 2가지 비교; 주요 시술은 발치.

🟡 조건부 반박radiology

PROSPERO 등록 SR+MA (관찰 연구 5편, 2001–2021) — 구내 bitewing/치근단 영상의 해석적 광학 아티팩트 (삼각형 방사선투과상 Triangular-Shaped Radiolucency, TSR; 마하밴드 Mach band; 치경부 소각 cervical burnout) 분석.

SR+MA (51편, 2000–2019): 영구치·유치 교합면·인접면별 탐지도 정량 — 탐지 방법 간 Se/Sp를 QUADAS-2로 평가.
🔴 정면충돌radiology

Systematic review of 5 phantom studies (PRISMA; 653 records screened, 2010–2022) quantifying scattered radiation dose to the eye lens from dental CBCT examinations.
2010~2022년 팬텀 연구 5편에 대한 체계적 문헌고찰(PRISMA; 653편 → 5편): 치과 CBCT 안구 수정체 산란선량을 정량화.

🟡 조건부 반박radiology

In-vitro Monte Carlo study (PHITS code) using a step-and-shoot method with a virtual Rando phantom to estimate effective dose for large and small FOV CBCT scans on a 3DX Accuitomo FPD8, validated against TLD measurements.

2989명(고혈압군 1440명 vs 비고혈압 대조군 1549명)을 대상으로 하악 전치부 치근단방사선사진(IOPAR)에서 영양관(Nutrient Canal, NC) 유무를 치주 골소실 상태·고혈압 중증도·이환기간과 함께 평가한 단면연구다.
